FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\database\DB_Khung_1.sql

-- ============================================================
-- UTEFIT DATABASE
-- FILE: 01_CREATE_DATABASE.sql
-- MODE: RESET DATABASE COMPLETELY
-- ============================================================

USE master;
GO

-- Xoa database cu neu ton tai
IF DB_ID(N'UTEFitDB') IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
    PRINT 'Dropping existing UTEFitDB database...';

    -- Ngat tat ca ket noi dang su dung database
    ALTER DATABASE UTEFitDB
    SET SINGLE_USER
    WITH ROLLBACK IMMEDIATE;

    -- Xoa toan bo database cu
    DROP DATABASE UTEFitDB;

    PRINT 'Old database deleted successfully.';
END
GO

-- Tao database moi
CREATE DATABASE UTEFitDB;
GO

USE UTEFitDB;
GO

PRINT 'New UTEFitDB database created successfully.';
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 1. USERS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.users (
                           id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                           avatar_public_id VARCHAR(255) NULL,
                           avatar_url VARCHAR(255) NULL,
                           created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                           email VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                           enabled BIT NOT NULL,
                           first_name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                           last_name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                           password VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                           phone VARCHAR(20) NULL,
                           role VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
                           status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
                           token_version INT NULL,
                           updated_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,

                           CONSTRAINT PK_users PRIMARY KEY (id),
                           CONSTRAINT UK_users_email UNIQUE (email),
                           CONSTRAINT CK_users_role CHECK (
                               role IN ('MEMBER', 'PT', 'ADMIN')
                               ),
                           CONSTRAINT CK_users_status CHECK (
                               status IN ('UNVERIFIED', 'ACTIVE', 'LOCKED')
                               )
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 2. BRANCHES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.branches (
                              id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                              active BIT NOT NULL,
                              address VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                              closing_time TIME NULL,
                              created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                              description VARCHAR(1000) NULL,
                              image_public_id VARCHAR(255) NULL,
                              image_url VARCHAR(255) NULL,
                              name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
                              opening_time TIME NULL,
                              phone VARCHAR(20) NULL,
                              updated_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,

                              CONSTRAINT PK_branches PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 3. CLASS CATEGORIES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.class_categories (
                                      id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                                      active BIT NOT NULL,
                                      created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                                      description VARCHAR(1000) NULL,
                                      name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                                      updated_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,

                                      CONSTRAINT PK_class_categories PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 4. MEMBERSHIP PLANS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.membership_plans (
                                      id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                                      active BIT NOT NULL,
                                      created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                                      description VARCHAR(1500) NULL,
                                      duration_days INT NOT NULL,
                                      name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                                      price NUMERIC(15,2) NOT NULL,
                                      updated_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,

                                      CONSTRAINT PK_membership_plans PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 5. TRAINERS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.trainers (
                              id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                              active BIT NOT NULL,
                              bio VARCHAR(2000) NULL,
                              created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                              specialization VARCHAR(255) NULL,
                              updated_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,
                              years_experience INT NULL,
                              branch_id BIGINT NULL,
                              user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,

                              CONSTRAINT PK_trainers PRIMARY KEY (id),
                              CONSTRAINT uk_trainers_user UNIQUE (user_id),

                              CONSTRAINT fk_trainer_branch
                                  FOREIGN KEY (branch_id)
                                      REFERENCES dbo.branches(id),

                              CONSTRAINT fk_trainer_user
                                  FOREIGN KEY (user_id)
                                      REFERENCES dbo.users(id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 6. FITNESS CLASSES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.fitness_classes (
                                     id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                                     capacity INT NOT NULL,
                                     created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                                     description VARCHAR(1500) NULL,
                                     name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
                                     status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
                                     updated_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,
                                     branch_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
                                     category_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
                                     trainer_id BIGINT NULL,

                                     CONSTRAINT PK_fitness_classes PRIMARY KEY (id),

                                     CONSTRAINT CK_fitness_classes_status CHECK (
                                         status IN ('OPEN', 'FULL', 'CANCELLED', 'COMPLETED')
                                         ),

                                     CONSTRAINT fk_class_branch
                                         FOREIGN KEY (branch_id)
                                             REFERENCES dbo.branches(id),

                                     CONSTRAINT fk_class_category
                                         FOREIGN KEY (category_id)
                                             REFERENCES dbo.class_categories(id),

                                     CONSTRAINT fk_class_trainer
                                         FOREIGN KEY (trainer_id)
                                             REFERENCES dbo.trainers(id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 7. CLASS SCHEDULES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.class_schedules (
                                     id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                                     active BIT NOT NULL,
                                     created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                                     end_time TIME NOT NULL,
                                     room VARCHAR(100) NULL,
                                     schedule_date DATE NOT NULL,
                                     start_time TIME NOT NULL,
                                     fitness_class_id BIGINT NOT NULL,

                                     CONSTRAINT PK_class_schedules PRIMARY KEY (id),

                                     CONSTRAINT fk_schedule_class
                                         FOREIGN KEY (fitness_class_id)
                                             REFERENCES dbo.fitness_classes(id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 8. CLASS REGISTRATIONS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.class_registrations (
                                         id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                                         registered_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                                         status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
                                         schedule_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
                                         user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,

                                         CONSTRAINT PK_class_registrations PRIMARY KEY (id),

                                         CONSTRAINT uk_registration_user_schedule
                                             UNIQUE (user_id, schedule_id),

                                         CONSTRAINT CK_class_registrations_status CHECK (
                                             status IN (
                                                        'REGISTERED',
                                                        'CANCELLED',
                                                        'ATTENDED',
                                                        'ABSENT'
                                                 )
                                             ),

                                         CONSTRAINT fk_registration_schedule
                                             FOREIGN KEY (schedule_id)
                                                 REFERENCES dbo.class_schedules(id),

                                         CONSTRAINT fk_registration_user
                                             FOREIGN KEY (user_id)
                                                 REFERENCES dbo.users(id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 9. MEMBERSHIPS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.memberships (
                                 id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                                 created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                                 end_date DATE NULL,
                                 start_date DATE NULL,
                                 status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
                                 updated_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,
                                 branch_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
                                 plan_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
                                 user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,

                                 CONSTRAINT PK_memberships PRIMARY KEY (id),

                                 CONSTRAINT CK_memberships_status CHECK (
                                     status IN (
                                                'PENDING',
                                                'ACTIVE',
                                                'EXPIRED',
                                                'CANCELLED'
                                         )
                                     ),

                                 CONSTRAINT fk_membership_branch
                                     FOREIGN KEY (branch_id)
                                         REFERENCES dbo.branches(id),

                                 CONSTRAINT fk_membership_plan
                                     FOREIGN KEY (plan_id)
                                         REFERENCES dbo.membership_plans(id),

                                 CONSTRAINT fk_membership_user
                                     FOREIGN KEY (user_id)
                                         REFERENCES dbo.users(id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 10. PAYMENTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.payments (
                              id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                              amount NUMERIC(15,2) NOT NULL,
                              created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                              method VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
                              paid_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,
                              status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
                              transaction_code VARCHAR(100) NULL,
                              membership_id BIGINT NULL,
                              user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,

                              CONSTRAINT PK_payments PRIMARY KEY (id),

                              CONSTRAINT CK_payments_method CHECK (
                                  method IN ('CASH', 'VNPAY')
                                  ),

                              CONSTRAINT CK_payments_status CHECK (
                                  status IN (
                                             'PENDING',
                                             'PAID',
                                             'FAILED',
                                             'REFUNDED'
                                      )
                                  ),

                              CONSTRAINT fk_payment_membership
                                  FOREIGN KEY (membership_id)
                                      REFERENCES dbo.memberships(id),

                              CONSTRAINT fk_payment_user
                                  FOREIGN KEY (user_id)
                                      REFERENCES dbo.users(id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 11. REVIEWS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.reviews (
                             id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                             comment VARCHAR(2000) NULL,
                             created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                             rating INT NOT NULL,
                             updated_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,
                             visible BIT NOT NULL,
                             branch_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
                             trainer_id BIGINT NULL,
                             user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,

                             CONSTRAINT PK_reviews PRIMARY KEY (id),

                             CONSTRAINT fk_review_branch
                                 FOREIGN KEY (branch_id)
                                     REFERENCES dbo.branches(id),

                             CONSTRAINT fk_review_trainer
                                 FOREIGN KEY (trainer_id)
                                     REFERENCES dbo.trainers(id),

                             CONSTRAINT fk_review_user
                                 FOREIGN KEY (user_id)
                                     REFERENCES dbo.users(id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 12. OTP CODES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.otp_codes (
                               id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                               attempt_count INT NULL,
                               code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                               created_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                               expired_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                               type VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
                               used BIT NOT NULL,
                               used_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,
                               user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,

                               CONSTRAINT PK_otp_codes PRIMARY KEY (id),

                               CONSTRAINT CK_otp_codes_type CHECK (
                                   type IN ('REGISTER', 'FORGOT_PASSWORD')
                                   ),

                               CONSTRAINT FK_otp_user
                                   FOREIGN KEY (user_id)
                                       REFERENCES dbo.users(id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- 13. TRAINER REQUESTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE dbo.trainer_requests (
                                      id BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
                                      message VARCHAR(2000) NULL,
                                      requested_at DATETIME2(7) NOT NULL,
                                      responded_at DATETIME2(7) NULL,
                                      status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
                                      member_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
                                      trainer_id BIGINT NOT NULL,

                                      CONSTRAINT PK_trainer_requests PRIMARY KEY (id),

                                      CONSTRAINT CK_trainer_requests_status CHECK (
                                          status IN ('PENDING', 'ACCEPTED', 'REJECTED')
                                          ),

                                      CONSTRAINT FK_trainer_requests_member
                                          FOREIGN KEY (member_id)
                                              REFERENCES dbo.users(id),

                                      CONSTRAINT FK_trainer_requests_trainer
                                          FOREIGN KEY (trainer_id)
                                              REFERENCES dbo.trainers(id)
);
GO

-- ============================================================
-- VERIFY DATABASE STRUCTURE
-- ============================================================

DECLARE @tableCount INT;

SELECT @tableCount = COUNT(*)
FROM sys.tables
WHERE schema_id = SCHEMA_ID('dbo');

IF @tableCount <> 13
BEGIN
    THROW 50001, 'Database must contain exactly 13 tables.', 1;
END;

PRINT '============================================';
PRINT 'UTEFIT DATABASE CREATED SUCCESSFULLY';
PRINT 'TOTAL TABLES: 13';
PRINT '============================================';
GO

ALTER TABLE [dbo].[fitness_classes]  WITH CHECK ADD CHECK  (([status]='COMPLETED' OR [status]='CANCELLED' OR [status]='FULL' OR [status]='OPEN'))
    GO
ALTER TABLE [dbo].[memberships]  WITH CHECK ADD CHECK  (([status]='CANCELLED' OR [status]='EXPIRED' OR [status]='ACTIVE' OR [status]='PENDING'))
    GO
ALTER TABLE [dbo].[otp_codes]  WITH CHECK ADD CHECK  (([type]='FORGOT_PASSWORD' OR [type]='REGISTER'))
    GO
ALTER TABLE [dbo].[payments]  WITH CHECK ADD CHECK  (([method]='VNPAY' OR [method]='CASH'))
    GO
ALTER TABLE [dbo].[payments]  WITH CHECK ADD CHECK  (([status]='REFUNDED' OR [status]='FAILED' OR [status]='PAID' OR [status]='PENDING'))
    GO
ALTER TABLE [dbo].[users]  WITH CHECK ADD CHECK  (([role]='ADMIN' OR [role]='PT' OR [role]='MEMBER'))
    GO
ALTER TABLE [dbo].[users]  WITH CHECK ADD CHECK  (([status]='LOCKED' OR [status]='ACTIVE' OR [status]='UNVERIFIED'))
    GO
    USE [master]
    GO
ALTER DATABASE [UTEFitDB] SET  READ_WRITE 
GO

package com.utefit.config;

import com.cloudinary.Cloudinary;
import com.cloudinary.utils.ObjectUtils;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

import java.util.Map;

@Configuration
public class CloudinaryConfig {

    @Value("${cloudinary.cloud-name}")
    private String cloudName;

    @Value("${cloudinary.api-key}")
    private String apiKey;

    @Value("${cloudinary.api-secret}")
    private String apiSecret;

    @Bean
    public Cloudinary cloudinary() {
        Map<String, Object> config = ObjectUtils.asMap(
                "cloud_name", cloudName,
                "api_key", apiKey,
                "api_secret", apiSecret,
                "secure", true
        );
        return new Cloudinary(config);
    }
}

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\config\SecurityConfig.java
============================================================

package com.utefit.config;

import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;
import com.utefit.security.CustomUserDetailsService;
import com.utefit.security.JwtAuthenticationFilter;
import com.utefit.security.JwtService;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.AuthenticationManager;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.ProviderManager;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.dao.DaoAuthenticationProvider;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UserDetailsService;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.security.web.SecurityFilterChain;
import org.springframework.security.web.authentication.LoginUrlAuthenticationEntryPoint;
import org.springframework.security.web.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter;
import org.springframework.security.web.csrf.HttpSessionCsrfTokenRepository;

@Configuration
public class SecurityConfig {

    @Bean
    public PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder() {
        return new BCryptPasswordEncoder();
    }

    @Bean
    public AuthenticationManager authenticationManager(
            UserDetailsService userDetailsService,
            PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder
    ) {
        DaoAuthenticationProvider provider =
                new DaoAuthenticationProvider(userDetailsService);

        provider.setPasswordEncoder(passwordEncoder);

        return new ProviderManager(provider);
    }

    @Bean
    public SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(
            HttpSecurity http,
            JwtService jwtService,
            UserRepository userRepository,
            CustomUserDetailsService userDetailsService
    ) throws Exception {

        JwtAuthenticationFilter jwtFilter =
                new JwtAuthenticationFilter(
                        jwtService,
                        userRepository,
                        userDetailsService
                );

        http
                // Không tắt CSRF vì JWT nằm trong cookie.
                .csrf(csrf -> csrf
                        .csrfTokenRepository(
                                new HttpSessionCsrfTokenRepository()
                        )
                )

                .authorizeHttpRequests(auth -> auth

                        .requestMatchers(
                                "/",
                                "/register",
                                "/verify-otp",
                                "/resend-otp",
                                "/login",
                                "/forgot-password",
                                "/reset-password",
                                "/access-denied",

                                "/css/**",
                                "/js/**",
                                "/images/**",
                                "/vendor/**",
                                "/error",
                                "/error/**",

                                // Guest - Giai đoạn 3
                                "/branches",
                                "/branches/**",
                                "/membership-plans",
                                "/membership-plans/**",
                                "/classes",
                                "/classes/**",

                                // Public PT của B
                                "/trainers",
                                "/trainers/**"
                        ).permitAll()

                        .requestMatchers("/member/**")
                        .hasRole("MEMBER")

                        .requestMatchers("/pt/**")
                        .hasRole("PT")

                        .requestMatchers("/admin/**")
                        .hasRole("ADMIN")

                        .requestMatchers("/auth/logout")
                        .authenticated()

                        .anyRequest()
                        .denyAll()
                )

                .exceptionHandling(ex -> ex
                        .authenticationEntryPoint(
                                new LoginUrlAuthenticationEntryPoint(
                                        "/login"
                                )
                        )
                        .accessDeniedHandler(
                                (request, response, exception) ->
                                        response.sendError(
                                                HttpServletResponse.SC_FORBIDDEN
                                        )
                        )
                )

                .formLogin(form -> form.disable())
                .httpBasic(basic -> basic.disable())
                .logout(logout -> logout.disable())

                .addFilterBefore(
                        jwtFilter,
                        UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter.class
                );

        return http.build();
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.admin;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.BranchFormDto;
import com.utefit.entity.Branch;
import com.utefit.service.AdminBranchService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.validation.BindingResult;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/admin/branches")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminBranchController {

    private final AdminBranchService adminBranchService;

    @GetMapping
    public String listBranches(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Boolean active,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "8") int size,
            Model model
    ) {
        Page<Branch> branchPage = adminBranchService.getBranches(keyword, active, page, size);

        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "branches");
        model.addAttribute("branchPage", branchPage);
        model.addAttribute("keyword", keyword);
        model.addAttribute("selectedActive", active);
        return "admin/branches/list";
    }

    @GetMapping("/create")
    public String showCreateForm(Model model) {
        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "branches");
        model.addAttribute("branchForm", new BranchFormDto());
        model.addAttribute("isEdit", false);
        return "admin/branches/form";
    }

    @PostMapping("/create")
    public String createBranch(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("branchForm") BranchFormDto branchForm,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes ra
    ) {
        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "branches");
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", false);
            return "admin/branches/form";
        }
        try {
            adminBranchService.createBranch(branchForm);
            ra.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Thêm chi nhánh mới thành công!");
            return "redirect:/admin/branches";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "branches");
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", false);
            model.addAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            return "admin/branches/form";
        }
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}/edit")
    public String showEditForm(@PathVariable Long id, Model model, RedirectAttributes ra) {
        try {
            BranchFormDto form = adminBranchService.getBranchFormById(id);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "branches");
            model.addAttribute("branchForm", form);
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", true);
            return "admin/branches/form";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            ra.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            return "redirect:/admin/branches";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/edit")
    public String updateBranch(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("branchForm") BranchFormDto branchForm,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes ra
    ) {
        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "branches");
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", true);
            return "admin/branches/form";
        }
        try {
            adminBranchService.updateBranch(id, branchForm);
            ra.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Cập nhật chi nhánh thành công!");
            return "redirect:/admin/branches";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "branches");
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", true);
            model.addAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            return "admin/branches/form";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/toggle-active")
    public String toggleActive(@PathVariable Long id, RedirectAttributes ra) {
        try {
            adminBranchService.toggleActive(id);
            ra.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Đã đổi trạng thái hoạt động của chi nhánh!");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            ra.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
        }
        return "redirect:/admin/branches";
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.admin;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.ClassCategoryFormDto;
import com.utefit.entity.ClassCategory;
import com.utefit.service.AdminCategoryService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.validation.BindingResult;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/admin/categories")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminCategoryController {

    private final AdminCategoryService categoryService;

    @GetMapping
    public String listCategories(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Boolean active,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "8") int size,
            Model model
    ) {
        Page<ClassCategory> categoryPage = categoryService.getCategories(keyword, active, page, size);

        model.addAttribute("categoryPage", categoryPage);
        model.addAttribute("keyword", keyword);
        model.addAttribute("selectedActive", active);
        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "categories");

        return "admin/categories/list";
    }

    @GetMapping("/create")
    public String showCreateForm(Model model) {
        model.addAttribute("categoryForm", new ClassCategoryFormDto());
        model.addAttribute("isEdit", false);
        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "categories");
        return "admin/categories/form";
    }

    @PostMapping("/create")
    public String createCategory(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("categoryForm") ClassCategoryFormDto categoryForm,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", false);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "categories");
            return "admin/categories/form";
        }

        try {
            categoryService.createCategory(categoryForm);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Tạo loại lớp tập mới thành công!");
            return "redirect:/admin/categories";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            model.addAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", false);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "categories");
            return "admin/categories/form";
        }
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}/edit")
    public String showEditForm(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        try {
            ClassCategoryFormDto categoryForm = categoryService.getCategoryFormById(id);
            model.addAttribute("categoryForm", categoryForm);
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", true);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "categories");
            return "admin/categories/form";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            return "redirect:/admin/categories";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/edit")
    public String updateCategory(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("categoryForm") ClassCategoryFormDto categoryForm,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        categoryForm.setId(id);
        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", true);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "categories");
            return "admin/categories/form";
        }

        try {
            categoryService.updateCategory(id, categoryForm);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Cập nhật loại lớp tập thành công!");
            return "redirect:/admin/categories";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            model.addAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", true);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "categories");
            return "admin/categories/form";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/toggle-active")
    public String toggleActive(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        try {
            categoryService.toggleActive(id);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Đã cập nhật trạng thái loại lớp tập!");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
        }
        return "redirect:/admin/categories";
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.admin;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.FitnessClassFormDto;
import com.utefit.entity.FitnessClass;
import com.utefit.enums.ClassStatus;
import com.utefit.service.AdminClassService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.validation.BindingResult;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/admin/classes")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminClassController {

    private final AdminClassService adminClassService;

    @GetMapping
    public String listClasses(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long branchId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long categoryId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long trainerId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) ClassStatus status,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "8") int size,
            Model model
    ) {
        Page<FitnessClass> classPage = adminClassService.getClasses(
                keyword, branchId, categoryId, trainerId, status, page, size
        );

        model.addAttribute("classPage", classPage);
        model.addAttribute("branches", adminClassService.getActiveBranches());
        model.addAttribute("categories", adminClassService.getActiveCategories());
        model.addAttribute("trainers", adminClassService.getActiveTrainers());
        model.addAttribute("statuses", ClassStatus.values());

        model.addAttribute("keyword", keyword);
        model.addAttribute("selectedBranchId", branchId);
        model.addAttribute("selectedCategoryId", categoryId);
        model.addAttribute("selectedTrainerId", trainerId);
        model.addAttribute("selectedStatus", status);
        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "classes");

        return "admin/classes/list";
    }

    @GetMapping("/create")
    public String showCreateForm(Model model) {
        model.addAttribute("classForm", new FitnessClassFormDto());
        populateFormOptions(model, false);
        return "admin/classes/form";
    }

    @PostMapping("/create")
    public String createClass(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("classForm") FitnessClassFormDto classForm,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            populateFormOptions(model, false);
            return "admin/classes/form";
        }

        try {
            adminClassService.createClass(classForm);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Tạo lớp tập mới thành công!");
            return "redirect:/admin/classes";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            model.addAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            populateFormOptions(model, false);
            return "admin/classes/form";
        }
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}/edit")
    public String showEditForm(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        try {
            FitnessClassFormDto classForm = adminClassService.getClassFormById(id);
            model.addAttribute("classForm", classForm);
            populateFormOptions(model, true);
            return "admin/classes/form";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            return "redirect:/admin/classes";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/edit")
    public String updateClass(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("classForm") FitnessClassFormDto classForm,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        classForm.setId(id);
        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            populateFormOptions(model, true);
            return "admin/classes/form";
        }

        try {
            adminClassService.updateClass(id, classForm);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Cập nhật thông tin lớp tập thành công!");
            return "redirect:/admin/classes";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            model.addAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            populateFormOptions(model, true);
            return "admin/classes/form";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/cancel")
    public String cancelClass(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        try {
            adminClassService.cancelClass(id);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute(
                    "successMessage",
                    "Đã chuyển lớp tập sang trạng thái HỦY (CANCELLED) và đóng các lịch học liên quan!"
            );
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
        }
        return "redirect:/admin/classes";
    }

    private void populateFormOptions(Model model, boolean isEdit) {
        model.addAttribute("branches", adminClassService.getActiveBranches());
        model.addAttribute("categories", adminClassService.getActiveCategories());
        model.addAttribute("trainers", adminClassService.getActiveTrainers());
        model.addAttribute("statuses", ClassStatus.values());
        model.addAttribute("isEdit", isEdit);
        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "classes");
    }
}

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/admin")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminDashboardController {

    private final AdminDashboardService adminDashboardService;

    @GetMapping({"", "/", "/dashboard"})

        model.addAttribute("totalBranches", kpis.get("totalBranches"));
        model.addAttribute("totalTrainers", kpis.get("totalTrainers"));

package com.utefit.controller.admin;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.MembershipPlanFormDto;
import com.utefit.entity.MembershipPlan;
import com.utefit.service.AdminMembershipPlanService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.validation.BindingResult;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/admin/membership-plans")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminMembershipPlanController {

    private final AdminMembershipPlanService planService;

    @GetMapping
    public String listPlans(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Boolean active,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "8") int size,
            Model model
    ) {
        Page<MembershipPlan> planPage = planService.getPlans(keyword, active, page, size);

        model.addAttribute("planPage", planPage);
        model.addAttribute("keyword", keyword);
        model.addAttribute("selectedActive", active);
        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "plans");

        return "admin/plans/list";
    }

    @GetMapping("/create")
    public String showCreateForm(Model model) {
        model.addAttribute("planForm", new MembershipPlanFormDto());
        model.addAttribute("isEdit", false);
        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "plans");
        return "admin/plans/form";
    }

    @PostMapping("/create")
    public String createPlan(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("planForm") MembershipPlanFormDto planForm,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", false);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "plans");
            return "admin/plans/form";
        }

        try {
            planService.createPlan(planForm);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Tạo gói tập mới thành công!");
            return "redirect:/admin/membership-plans";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            model.addAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", false);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "plans");
            return "admin/plans/form";
        }
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}/edit")
    public String showEditForm(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        try {
            MembershipPlanFormDto planForm = planService.getPlanFormById(id);
            model.addAttribute("planForm", planForm);
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", true);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "plans");
            return "admin/plans/form";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            return "redirect:/admin/membership-plans";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/edit")
    public String updatePlan(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("planForm") MembershipPlanFormDto planForm,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        planForm.setId(id);
        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", true);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "plans");
            return "admin/plans/form";
        }

        try {
            planService.updatePlan(id, planForm);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Cập nhật gói tập thành công!");
            return "redirect:/admin/membership-plans";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            model.addAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            model.addAttribute("isEdit", true);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "plans");
            return "admin/plans/form";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/toggle-active")
    public String toggleActive(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        try {
            planService.toggleActive(id);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Đã cập nhật trạng thái gói tập!");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
        }
        return "redirect:/admin/membership-plans";
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.admin;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.ClassScheduleFormDto;
import com.utefit.entity.ClassSchedule;
import com.utefit.service.AdminScheduleService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.format.annotation.DateTimeFormat;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.validation.BindingResult;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

import java.time.LocalDate;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/admin/schedules")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminScheduleController {

    private final AdminScheduleService adminScheduleService;

    @GetMapping
    public String listSchedules(
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long fitnessClassId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long branchId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) @DateTimeFormat(pattern = "yyyy-MM-dd") LocalDate scheduleDate,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Boolean active,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "8") int size,
            Model model
    ) {
        Page<ClassSchedule> schedulePage = adminScheduleService.getSchedules(
                fitnessClassId, branchId, scheduleDate, active, page, size
        );

        model.addAttribute("schedulePage", schedulePage);
        model.addAttribute("classes", adminScheduleService.getSchedulableClasses());
        model.addAttribute("branches", adminScheduleService.getAllBranches());

        model.addAttribute("selectedClassId", fitnessClassId);
        model.addAttribute("selectedBranchId", branchId);
        model.addAttribute("selectedDate", scheduleDate);
        model.addAttribute("selectedActive", active);
        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "schedules");

        return "admin/schedules/list";
    }

    @GetMapping("/create")
    public String showCreateForm(
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long classId,
            Model model
    ) {
        ClassScheduleFormDto form = ClassScheduleFormDto.builder()
                .fitnessClassId(classId)
                .scheduleDate(LocalDate.now().plusDays(1))
                .active(true)
                .build();

        model.addAttribute("scheduleForm", form);
        populateFormOptions(model, false);
        return "admin/schedules/form";
    }

    @PostMapping("/create")
    public String createSchedule(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("scheduleForm") ClassScheduleFormDto scheduleForm,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            populateFormOptions(model, false);
            return "admin/schedules/form";
        }

        try {
            adminScheduleService.createSchedule(scheduleForm);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Xếp lịch học mới thành công!");
            return "redirect:/admin/schedules";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            model.addAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            populateFormOptions(model, false);
            return "admin/schedules/form";
        }
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}/edit")
    public String showEditForm(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        try {
            ClassScheduleFormDto scheduleForm = adminScheduleService.getScheduleFormById(id);
            model.addAttribute("scheduleForm", scheduleForm);
            populateFormOptions(model, true);
            return "admin/schedules/form";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            return "redirect:/admin/schedules";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/edit")
    public String updateSchedule(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("scheduleForm") ClassScheduleFormDto scheduleForm,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        scheduleForm.setId(id);
        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            populateFormOptions(model, true);
            return "admin/schedules/form";
        }

        try {
            adminScheduleService.updateSchedule(id, scheduleForm);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Cập nhật lịch học thành công!");
            return "redirect:/admin/schedules";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            model.addAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            populateFormOptions(model, true);
            return "admin/schedules/form";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/toggle-active")
    public String toggleActive(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        try {
            adminScheduleService.toggleActive(id);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Đã cập nhật trạng thái buổi học!");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
        }
        return "redirect:/admin/schedules";
    }

    private void populateFormOptions(Model model, boolean isEdit) {
        model.addAttribute("classes", adminScheduleService.getSchedulableClasses());
        model.addAttribute("isEdit", isEdit);
        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "schedules");
    }
}

        return "redirect:/admin/trainers";
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.admin;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.AdminUserResponse;
import com.utefit.enums.UserRole;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import com.utefit.service.AdminUserService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

import java.security.Principal;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/admin/users")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminUserController {

    private final AdminUserService adminUserService;

    @GetMapping
    public String listUsers(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(required = false) UserRole role,
            @RequestParam(required = false) UserStatus status,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "8") int size,
            Model model
    ) {
        Page<AdminUserResponse> userPage = adminUserService.getUsers(keyword, role, status, page, size);

        model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "users");
        model.addAttribute("userPage", userPage);
        model.addAttribute("keyword", keyword);
        model.addAttribute("selectedRole", role);
        model.addAttribute("selectedStatus", status);
        model.addAttribute("roles", UserRole.values());
        model.addAttribute("statuses", UserStatus.values());
        return "admin/users/list";
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public String userDetail(@PathVariable Long id, Model model, RedirectAttributes ra) {
        try {
            AdminUserResponse user = adminUserService.getUserById(id);
            model.addAttribute("activeMenu", "users");
            model.addAttribute("user", user);
            return "admin/users/detail";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            ra.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            return "redirect:/admin/users";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/toggle-lock")
    public String toggleLock(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            Principal principal,
            RedirectAttributes ra
    ) {
        try {
            String currentEmail = (principal != null) ? principal.getName() : null;
            adminUserService.toggleLockUser(id, currentEmail);
            ra.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Cập nhật trạng thái tài khoản thành công!");
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ra.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
        }
        return "redirect:/admin/users";
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.admin;

import com.utefit.service.MembershipPaymentService;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/admin/payments")
public class CashPaymentAdminController {

    private final MembershipPaymentService billing;

    public CashPaymentAdminController(
            MembershipPaymentService billing
    ) {
        this.billing = billing;
    }

    @GetMapping({"", "/cash-pending"})
    public String pendingCash(Model model) {

        model.addAttribute(
                "activeMenu",
                "payments"
        );

        model.addAttribute(
                "payments",
                billing.pendingCashPayments()
        );

        return "admin/payments/cash-pending";
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/confirm-cash")
    public String confirmCash(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            RedirectAttributes redirect
    ) {
        try {
            billing.confirmCashPayment(id);

            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "success",
                    "Đã xác nhận thu tiền. "
                            + "Membership chuyển sang ACTIVE."
            );

        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "error",
                    e.getMessage()
            );
        }

        return "redirect:/admin/payments";
    }
}

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\controller\auth\AuthController.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.controller.auth;

import com.utefit.dto.auth.*;
import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.service.AuthService;
import com.utefit.security.JwtService;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseCookie;

import org.springframework.security.core.AuthenticationException;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.validation.BindingResult;

import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;
import org.springframework.mail.MailException;

import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;

@Controller
public class AuthController {

    private final AuthService auth;
    private final JwtService jwt;
    private final boolean secureCookie;

    public AuthController(
            AuthService auth,
            JwtService jwt,
            @Value("${utefit.jwt.cookie-secure:false}")
            boolean secureCookie
    ) {
        this.auth = auth;
        this.jwt = jwt;
        this.secureCookie = secureCookie;
    }

    private String q(String value) {
        return URLEncoder.encode(
                value,
                StandardCharsets.UTF_8
        );
    }

    // =========================
    // REGISTER
    // =========================

    @GetMapping("/register")
    public String registerPage(Model model) {

        if (!model.containsAttribute("form")) {
            model.addAttribute(
                    "form",
                    new RegisterRequest()
            );
        }

        return "auth/register";
    }

    @PostMapping("/register")
    public String register(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("form") RegisterRequest form,
            BindingResult errors
    ) {
        if (errors.hasErrors()) {
            return "auth/register";
        }

        try {
            String email = auth.register(form);

            return "redirect:/verify-otp?email="
                    + q(email);

        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {

            errors.reject(
                    "register",
                    ex.getMessage()
            );

            return "auth/register";
        }
    }

    // =========================
    // VERIFY OTP
    // =========================

    @GetMapping("/verify-otp")
    public String verifyPage(
            @RequestParam(
                    required = false,
                    defaultValue = ""
            )
            String email,
            Model model
    ) {
        if (!model.containsAttribute("form")) {

            VerifyOtpRequest form =
                    new VerifyOtpRequest();

            form.setEmail(email);

            model.addAttribute("form", form);
        }

        return "auth/verify-otp";
    }

    @PostMapping("/verify-otp")
    public String verify(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("form")
            VerifyOtpRequest form,
            BindingResult errors,
            RedirectAttributes redirect
    ) {
        if (errors.hasErrors()) {
            return "auth/verify-otp";
        }

        try {
            auth.verifyRegistration(form);

            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "success",
                    "Xác thực thành công, hãy đăng nhập."
            );

            return "redirect:/login";

        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {

            errors.reject(
                    "otp",
                    ex.getMessage()
            );

            return "auth/verify-otp";
        }
    }

    // =========================
    // RESEND OTP
    // =========================

    @PostMapping("/resend-otp")
    public String resend(
            @RequestParam String email,
            RedirectAttributes redirect
    ) {
        try {
            auth.resendRegistrationOtp(email);

            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "success",
                    "Nếu tài khoản đang chờ xác thực, OTP mới đã gửi."
            );

        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {

            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "error",
                    ex.getMessage()
            );
        }

        return "redirect:/verify-otp?email="
                + q(email);
    }

    // =========================
    // LOGIN
    // =========================

    @GetMapping("/login")
    public String loginPage(Model model) {

        if (!model.containsAttribute("form")) {
            model.addAttribute(
                    "form",
                    new LoginRequest()
            );
        }

        return "auth/login";
    }

    @PostMapping("/login")
    public String login(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("form") LoginRequest form,
            BindingResult errors,
            HttpServletResponse response
    ) {
        if (errors.hasErrors()) {
            return "auth/login";
        }

        try {
            User user = auth.authenticate(
                    form.getEmail(),
                    form.getPassword()
            );

            String token = jwt.generate(user);

            ResponseCookie cookie =
                    ResponseCookie.from(
                                    "UTEFIT_AUTH",
                                    token
                            )
                            .httpOnly(true)
                            .secure(secureCookie)
                            .sameSite("Lax")
                            .path("/")
                            .maxAge(Duration.ofHours(2))
                            .build();

            response.addHeader(
                    HttpHeaders.SET_COOKIE,
                    cookie.toString()
            );

            return "redirect:" + switch (user.getRole()) {
                case MEMBER -> "/member/dashboard";
                case PT -> "/pt/dashboard";
                case ADMIN -> "/admin/dashboard";
            };

        } catch (AuthenticationException ex) {

            errors.reject(
                    "login",
                    "Sai email/mật khẩu hoặc tài khoản chưa kích hoạt."
            );

            return "auth/login";
        }
    }

    // =========================
    // LOGOUT
    // =========================

    @PostMapping("/auth/logout")
    public String logout(
            Authentication principal,
            HttpServletResponse response
    ) {
        if (principal != null && principal.isAuthenticated()) {
            auth.revokeTokensForUser(principal.getName());
        }

        ResponseCookie cookie = ResponseCookie.from("UTEFIT_AUTH", "")
                .httpOnly(true)
                .secure(secureCookie)
                .sameSite("Lax")
                .path("/")
                .maxAge(Duration.ZERO)
                .build();

        response.addHeader(
                HttpHeaders.SET_COOKIE,
                cookie.toString()
        );

        return "redirect:/login";
    }

    // =========================
    // FORGOT PASSWORD
    // =========================

    @GetMapping("/forgot-password")
    public String forgotPage(Model model) {

        if (!model.containsAttribute("form")) {

            model.addAttribute(
                    "form",
                    new ForgotPasswordRequest()
            );
        }

        return "auth/forgot-password";
    }


    @PostMapping("/forgot-password")
    public String forgot(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("form")
            ForgotPasswordRequest form,
            BindingResult errors,
            RedirectAttributes redirect
    ) {
        if (errors.hasErrors()) {
            return "auth/forgot-password";
        }

        try {
            auth.requestPasswordReset(
                    form.getEmail()
            );

            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "success",
                    "Nếu tài khoản hợp lệ, mã OTP sẽ được gửi đến email."
            );

            return "redirect:/reset-password?email="
                    + q(form.getEmail());

        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {

            errors.reject(
                    "forgot",
                    ex.getMessage()
            );

            return "auth/forgot-password";

        } catch (MailException ex) {

            errors.reject(
                    "mail",
                    "Không gửi được email OTP. Vui lòng thử lại sau."
            );

            return "auth/forgot-password";
        }
    }


    // =========================
    // RESET PASSWORD
    // =========================

    @GetMapping("/reset-password")
    public String resetPage(
            @RequestParam(
                    required = false,
                    defaultValue = ""
            )
            String email,
            Model model
    ) {
        if (!model.containsAttribute("form")) {

            ResetPasswordRequest form =
                    new ResetPasswordRequest();

            form.setEmail(email);

            model.addAttribute("form", form);
        }

        return "auth/reset-password";
    }

    @PostMapping("/reset-password")
    public String reset(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("form")
            ResetPasswordRequest form,
            BindingResult errors,
            RedirectAttributes redirect
    ) {
        if (errors.hasErrors()) {
            return "auth/reset-password";
        }

        try {
            auth.resetPassword(form);

            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "success",
                    "Đổi mật khẩu thành công, hãy đăng nhập."
            );

            return "redirect:/login";

        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {

            errors.reject(
                    "reset",
                    ex.getMessage()
            );

            return "auth/reset-password";
        }
    }

    // =========================
    // ACCESS DENIED
    // =========================

    @GetMapping("/access-denied")
    public String accessDenied() {
        return "error/403";
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.guest;

import com.utefit.dto.trainer.TrainerSearchRequest;
import com.utefit.service.TrainerService;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;

import org.springframework.validation.BindingResult;

import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ModelAttribute;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/trainers")
public class TrainerPublicController {

    private final TrainerService trainerService;

    public TrainerPublicController(
            TrainerService trainerService
    ) {
        this.trainerService = trainerService;
    }

    @GetMapping
    public String list(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("search")
            TrainerSearchRequest request,
            BindingResult bindingResult,
            Model model
    ) {

        if (bindingResult.hasErrors()) {
            request = new TrainerSearchRequest();
        }

        model.addAttribute(
                "search",
                request
        );

        model.addAttribute(
                "trainerPage",
                trainerService.searchPublicTrainers(request)
        );

        model.addAttribute(
                "branches",
                trainerService.getActiveBranchOptions()
        );

        return "guest/trainers";
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public String detail(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            Model model
    ) {

        model.addAttribute(
                "trainer",
                trainerService.getPublicTrainerById(id)
        );

        return "guest/trainer-detail";
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.member;

import com.utefit.dto.member.MembershipPurchaseRequest;
import com.utefit.service.MembershipPaymentService;
import com.utefit.service.PublicCatalogService;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.validation.BindingResult;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

import java.security.Principal;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/member")
public class MemberMembershipController {

    private final MembershipPaymentService billing;
    private final PublicCatalogService catalog;

    public MemberMembershipController(
            MembershipPaymentService billing,
            PublicCatalogService catalog
    ) {
        this.billing = billing;
        this.catalog = catalog;
    }

    @GetMapping("/memberships")
    public String memberships(
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long branchId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long planId,
            Principal principal,
            Model model
    ) {
        MembershipPurchaseRequest form =
                new MembershipPurchaseRequest();

        // Có chọn gói từ trang trước thì chọn sẵn gói đó.
        if (planId != null && planId > 0) {
            form.setPlanId(planId);
        }

        // Có chọn chi nhánh từ trang trước thì chọn sẵn chi nhánh đó.
        if (branchId != null && branchId > 0) {
            form.setBranchId(branchId);
        }

        model.addAttribute("purchaseForm", form);

        prepareMembershipPage(principal, model);

        return "member/memberships";
    }

    @PostMapping("/memberships")
    public String purchase(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("purchaseForm")
            MembershipPurchaseRequest form,
            BindingResult result,
            Principal principal,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirect
    ) {
        if (result.hasErrors()) {
            prepareMembershipPage(principal, model);
            return "member/memberships";
        }

        try {
            billing.createMembership(
                    principal.getName(),
                    form
            );

            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "success",
                    "Đã tạo đăng ký gói tập PENDING. "
                            + "Hãy gửi yêu cầu thanh toán CASH."
            );
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "error",
                    e.getMessage()
            );
        }

        return "redirect:/member/memberships";
    }

    @PostMapping("/memberships/{id}/cash")
    public String requestCash(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            Principal principal,
            RedirectAttributes redirect
    ) {
        try {
            billing.requestCashPayment(
                    principal.getName(),
                    id
            );

            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "success",
                    "Đã tạo yêu cầu thanh toán CASH. "
                            + "Vui lòng thanh toán tại quầy "
                            + "và chờ Admin xác nhận."
            );
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "error",
                    e.getMessage()
            );
        }

        return "redirect:/member/memberships";
    }

    @GetMapping("/payments")
    public String paymentHistory(
            Principal principal,
            Model model
    ) {
        model.addAttribute(
                "payments",
                billing.myPayments(principal.getName())
        );

        return "member/payments";
    }

    private void prepareMembershipPage(
            Principal principal,
            Model model
    ) {
        model.addAttribute(
                "memberships",
                billing.myMemberships(principal.getName())
        );

        model.addAttribute(
                "branches",
                catalog.activeBranchOptions()
        );

        model.addAttribute(
                "plans",
                catalog.searchPlans(null, 0)
                        .getContent()
        );

        if (!model.containsAttribute("purchaseForm")) {
            model.addAttribute(
                    "purchaseForm",
                    new MembershipPurchaseRequest()
            );
        }
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.member;

import com.utefit.dto.member.PasswordChangeRequest;
import com.utefit.dto.member.ProfileUpdateRequest;
import com.utefit.service.MemberProfileService;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;

import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseCookie;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.validation.BindingResult;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

import java.security.Principal;
import java.time.Duration;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/member/profile")
public class MemberProfileController {

    private final MemberProfileService profiles;

    public MemberProfileController(
            MemberProfileService profiles
    ) {
        this.profiles = profiles;
    }

    @GetMapping
    public String showProfile(
            Principal principal,
            Model model
    ) {
        model.addAttribute(
                "profile",
                profiles.getProfile(principal.getName())
        );

        model.addAttribute(
                "profileForm",
                profiles.getEditForm(principal.getName())
        );

        model.addAttribute(
                "passwordForm",
                new PasswordChangeRequest()
        );

        return "member/profile";
    }

    @PostMapping
    public String updateProfile(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("profileForm")
            ProfileUpdateRequest form,
            BindingResult result,
            Principal principal,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirect
    ) {
        if (result.hasErrors()) {
            model.addAttribute(
                    "profile",
                    profiles.getProfile(principal.getName())
            );
            model.addAttribute(
                    "passwordForm",
                    new PasswordChangeRequest()
            );
            return "member/profile";
        }

        try {
            profiles.updateProfile(
                    principal.getName(),
                    form
            );
            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "success",
                    "Cập nhật thông tin thành công"
            );
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "error",
                    e.getMessage()
            );
        }

        return "redirect:/member/profile";
    }

    @PostMapping("/password")
    public String changePassword(
            @Valid @ModelAttribute("passwordForm")
            PasswordChangeRequest form,
            BindingResult result,
            Principal principal,
            Model model,
            HttpServletResponse response,
            RedirectAttributes redirect
    ) {
        if (result.hasErrors()) {
            model.addAttribute(
                    "profile",
                    profiles.getProfile(principal.getName())
            );
            model.addAttribute(
                    "profileForm",
                    profiles.getEditForm(principal.getName())
            );
            return "member/profile";
        }

        try {
            profiles.changePassword(
                    principal.getName(),
                    form
            );

            ResponseCookie cookie = ResponseCookie
                    .from("UTEFIT_AUTH", "")
                    .httpOnly(true)
                    .sameSite("Lax")
                    .path("/")
                    .maxAge(Duration.ZERO)
                    .build();

            response.addHeader(
                    HttpHeaders.SET_COOKIE,
                    cookie.toString()
            );

            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "success",
                    "Đổi mật khẩu thành công. Vui lòng đăng nhập lại."
            );

            return "redirect:/login";

        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "error",
                    e.getMessage()
            );
            return "redirect:/member/profile";
        }
    }

    @PostMapping("/avatar")
    public String changeAvatar(
            @RequestParam("avatar") MultipartFile file,
            Principal principal,
            RedirectAttributes redirect
    ) {
        try {
            profiles.updateAvatar(
                    principal.getName(),
                    file
            );
            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "success",
                    "Cập nhật ảnh đại diện thành công"
            );
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            redirect.addFlashAttribute(
                    "error",
                    e.getMessage()
            );
        }

        return "redirect:/member/profile";
    }
}
