package com.utefit.controller.member;

import com.utefit.dto.trainer.TrainerResponse;
import com.utefit.dto.trainerrequest.TrainerRequestResponse;
import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;
import com.utefit.service.TrainerRequestService;
import com.utefit.service.TrainerService;
import jakarta.persistence.EntityNotFoundException;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/member/trainer-requests")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class MemberTrainerRequestController {

    private final TrainerRequestService trainerRequestService;
    private final TrainerService trainerService;
    private final UserRepository userRepository;

    @GetMapping
    public String listMemberRequests(
            @RequestParam(value = "page", defaultValue = "0") int page,
            Model model
    ) {
        Long memberId = getCurrentUserId();

        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, 10);
        Page<TrainerRequestResponse> requestPage =
                trainerRequestService.getRequestsForMember(memberId, pageable);

        model.addAttribute("requestPage", requestPage);
        return "member/trainer-requests";
    }

    @GetMapping("/new")
    public String showCreateForm(
            @RequestParam("trainerId") Long trainerId,
            Model model,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        try {
            TrainerResponse trainer = trainerService.getPublicTrainerById(trainerId);
            model.addAttribute("trainer", trainer);
        } catch (EntityNotFoundException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            return "redirect:/trainers";
        }

        return "member/trainer-request-form";
    }

    @PostMapping
    public String createRequest(
            @RequestParam("trainerId") Long trainerId,
            @RequestParam(value = "message", required = false) String message,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        Long memberId = getCurrentUserId();

        try {
            trainerRequestService.createRequest(memberId, trainerId, message);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute(
                    "successMessage",
                    "Đã gửi yêu cầu tới huấn luyện viên."
            );
            return "redirect:/member/trainer-requests";
        } catch (IllegalStateException | EntityNotFoundException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
            return "redirect:/member/trainer-requests/new?trainerId=" + trainerId;
        }
    }

    private Long getCurrentUserId() {
        Authentication authentication =
                SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();

        if (authentication == null || !authentication.isAuthenticated()
                || "anonymousUser".equals(authentication.getPrincipal())) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Bạn cần đăng nhập để thực hiện thao tác này.");
        }

        String email = authentication.getName();

        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalStateException("Không tìm thấy tài khoản người dùng."));

        return user.getId();
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.member;

import com.utefit.service.PublicCatalogService;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

@Controller
public class PublicCatalogController {

    private final PublicCatalogService catalog;

    public PublicCatalogController(
            PublicCatalogService catalog
    ) {
        this.catalog = catalog;
    }

    @GetMapping("/branches")
    public String branches(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "") String keyword,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            Model model
    ) {
        model.addAttribute(
                "branchPage",
                catalog.searchBranches(keyword, page)
        );
        model.addAttribute("keyword", keyword);
        return "guest/branches";
    }

    @GetMapping("/branches/{id}")
    public String branchDetail(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            Model model
    ) {
        model.addAttribute(
                "branch",
                catalog.branchDetail(id)
        );
        return "guest/branch-detail";
    }

    @GetMapping("/membership-plans")
    public String plans(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "") String keyword,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            Model model
    ) {
        model.addAttribute(
                "planPage",
                catalog.searchPlans(keyword, page)
        );
        model.addAttribute("keyword", keyword);
        return "guest/membership-plans";
    }

    @GetMapping("/classes")
    public String classes(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "") String keyword,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long branchId,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            Model model
    ) {
        model.addAttribute(
                "classPage",
                catalog.searchClasses(keyword, branchId, page)
        );
        model.addAttribute(
                "branches",
                catalog.activeBranchOptions()
        );
        model.addAttribute("keyword", keyword);
        model.addAttribute("branchId", branchId);

        return "guest/classes";
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.pt;

import com.utefit.entity.Trainer;
import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.repository.TrainerRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.BranchRepository;
import com.utefit.service.CloudinaryService;
import com.utefit.dto.cloudinary.MediaUploadResult;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/pt/profile")
public class PtProfileController {

    private final TrainerRepository trainerRepository;
    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final BranchRepository branchRepository;
    private final CloudinaryService cloudinaryService;

    public PtProfileController(TrainerRepository trainerRepository,
                               UserRepository userRepository,
                               BranchRepository branchRepository,
                               CloudinaryService cloudinaryService) {
        this.trainerRepository = trainerRepository;
        this.userRepository = userRepository;
        this.branchRepository = branchRepository;
        this.cloudinaryService = cloudinaryService;
    }

    private Trainer getCurrentTrainer(Authentication authentication) {
        String email = authentication.getName();
        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Không tìm thấy tài khoản đăng nhập!"));

        return trainerRepository.findByUserId(user.getId())
                .orElseGet(() -> {
                    Trainer newTrainer = Trainer.builder()
                            .user(user)
                            .specialization("General Fitness")
                            .yearsExperience(1)
                            .bio("Chưa cập nhật tiểu sử.")
                            .active(true)
                            .build();
                    return trainerRepository.save(newTrainer);
                });
    }

    // /pt/profile
    @GetMapping
    public String viewProfile(Authentication authentication, Model model) {
        Trainer trainer = getCurrentTrainer(authentication);
        model.addAttribute("trainer", trainer);
        return "pt/profile/detail";
    }

    // /pt/profile/edit
    @GetMapping("/edit")
    public String editForm(Authentication authentication, Model model) {
        Trainer trainer = getCurrentTrainer(authentication);
        model.addAttribute("trainer", trainer);
        model.addAttribute("branches", branchRepository.findAll());
        return "pt/profile/edit";  // đúng với path bạn nói
    }

    @PostMapping("/edit")
    public String updateProfile(
            Authentication authentication,
            @RequestParam("specialization") String specialization,
            @RequestParam("yearsExperience") Integer yearsExperience,
            @RequestParam("bio") String bio,
            @RequestParam(value = "branchId", required = false) Long branchId,
            @RequestParam(value = "avatarFile", required = false) MultipartFile avatarFile,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        try {
            Trainer trainer = getCurrentTrainer(authentication);

            trainer.setSpecialization(specialization);
            trainer.setYearsExperience(yearsExperience);
            trainer.setBio(bio);

            if (branchId != null) {
                branchRepository.findById(branchId).ifPresent(trainer::setBranch);
            }

            if (avatarFile != null && !avatarFile.isEmpty()) {
                MediaUploadResult uploadResult = cloudinaryService.uploadImage(avatarFile, "avatars");

                if (uploadResult != null) {
                    String url = uploadResult.url();
                    String publicId = uploadResult.publicId();

                    trainer.getUser().setAvatarUrl(url);
                    trainer.getUser().setAvatarPublicId(publicId);
                    userRepository.save(trainer.getUser());
                }
            }

            trainerRepository.save(trainer);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("successMessage", "Cập nhật hồ sơ thành công!");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", "Có lỗi xảy ra: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        return "redirect:/pt/profile";
    }
}

package com.utefit.controller.pt;

import com.utefit.dto.trainerrequest.TrainerRequestResponse;
import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;
import com.utefit.service.TrainerRequestService;
import jakarta.persistence.EntityNotFoundException;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.support.RedirectAttributes;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/pt/trainer-requests")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class PtTrainerRequestController {

    private final TrainerRequestService trainerRequestService;
    private final UserRepository userRepository;

    @GetMapping
    public String listTrainerRequests(
            @RequestParam(value = "page", defaultValue = "0") int page,
            Model model
    ) {
        Long trainerUserId = getCurrentUserId();

        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, 10);
        Page<TrainerRequestResponse> requestPage =
                trainerRequestService.getRequestsForTrainer(trainerUserId, pageable);

        model.addAttribute("requestPage", requestPage);
        return "pt/trainer-requests";
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/accept")
    public String acceptRequest(
            @PathVariable("id") Long requestId,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        Long trainerUserId = getCurrentUserId();

        try {
            trainerRequestService.acceptRequest(trainerUserId, requestId);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute(
                    "successMessage",
                    "Đã chấp nhận yêu cầu."
            );
        } catch (IllegalStateException | EntityNotFoundException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
        }

        return "redirect:/pt/trainer-requests";
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/reject")
    public String rejectRequest(
            @PathVariable("id") Long requestId,
            RedirectAttributes redirectAttributes
    ) {
        Long trainerUserId = getCurrentUserId();

        try {
            trainerRequestService.rejectRequest(trainerUserId, requestId);
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute(
                    "successMessage",
                    "Đã từ chối yêu cầu."
            );
        } catch (IllegalStateException | EntityNotFoundException ex) {
            redirectAttributes.addFlashAttribute("errorMessage", ex.getMessage());
        }

        return "redirect:/pt/trainer-requests";
    }

    private Long getCurrentUserId() {
        Authentication authentication =
                SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();

        if (authentication == null || !authentication.isAuthenticated()
                || "anonymousUser".equals(authentication.getPrincipal())) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Bạn cần đăng nhập để thực hiện thao tác này.");
        }

        String email = authentication.getName();

        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalStateException("Không tìm thấy tài khoản người dùng."));

        return user.getId();
    }
}

package com.utefit.dto.admin;

import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.enums.UserRole;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import lombok.*;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class AdminUserResponse {
    private Long id;
    private String firstName;
    private String lastName;
    private String fullName;
    private String email;
    private String phone;
    private String avatarUrl;
    private UserRole role;
    private UserStatus status;
    private Boolean enabled;
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    public static AdminUserResponse fromEntity(User user) {
        return AdminUserResponse.builder()
                .id(user.getId())
                .firstName(user.getFirstName())
                .lastName(user.getLastName())
                .fullName(user.getLastName() + " " + user.getFirstName())
                .email(user.getEmail())
                .phone(user.getPhone())
                .avatarUrl(user.getAvatarUrl())
                .role(user.getRole())
                .status(user.getStatus())
                .enabled(user.getEnabled())
                .createdAt(user.getCreatedAt())
                .updatedAt(user.getUpdatedAt())
                .build();
    }
}

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\entity\Branch.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.LocalTime;

@Entity
@Table(name = "branches")
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class Branch {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 150)
    private String name;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 255)
    private String address;

    @Column(length = 20)
    private String phone;

    @Column(length = 1000)
    private String description;

    @Column(name = "image_url")
    private String imageUrl;

    @Column(name = "image_public_id")
    private String imagePublicId;

    @Column(name = "opening_time")
    private LocalTime openingTime;

    @Column(name = "closing_time")
    private LocalTime closingTime;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private Boolean active;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist
    public void prePersist() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

        createdAt = now;
        updatedAt = now;

        if (active == null) {
            active = true;
        }
    }

    @PreUpdate
    public void preUpdate() {
        updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\entity\ClassRegistration.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.entity;

import com.utefit.enums.RegistrationStatus;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Table(
        name = "class_registrations",
        uniqueConstraints = {
                @UniqueConstraint(
                        name = "uk_registration_user_schedule",
                        columnNames = {
                                "user_id",
                                "schedule_id"
                        }
                )
        }
)
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class ClassRegistration {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "user_id",
            nullable = false,
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_registration_user")
    )
    private User user;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "schedule_id",
            nullable = false,
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_registration_schedule")
    )
    private ClassSchedule schedule;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private RegistrationStatus status;

    @Column(name = "registered_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime registeredAt;

    @PrePersist
    public void prePersist() {
        if (registeredAt == null) {
            registeredAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        }

        if (status == null) {
            status = RegistrationStatus.REGISTERED;
        }
    }
}

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\entity\ClassSchedule.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.LocalTime;

@Entity
@Table(name = "class_schedules")
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class ClassSchedule {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "fitness_class_id",
            nullable = false,
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_schedule_class")
    )
    private FitnessClass fitnessClass;

    @Column(name = "schedule_date", nullable = false)
    private LocalDate scheduleDate;

    @Column(name = "start_time", nullable = false)
    private LocalTime startTime;

    @Column(name = "end_time", nullable = false)
    private LocalTime endTime;

    @Column(length = 100)
    private String room;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private Boolean active;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @PrePersist
    public void prePersist() {
        if (createdAt == null) {
            createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        }

        if (active == null) {
            active = true;
        }
    }
}

package com.utefit.entity;

import com.utefit.enums.ClassStatus;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Table(name = "fitness_classes")
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class FitnessClass {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 150)
    private String name;

    @Column(length = 1500)
    private String description;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "branch_id",
            nullable = false,
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_class_branch")
    )
    private Branch branch;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "category_id",
            nullable = false,
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_class_category")
    )
    private ClassCategory category;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "trainer_id",
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_class_trainer")
    )
    private Trainer trainer;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private Integer capacity;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private ClassStatus status;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist
    public void prePersist() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

        createdAt = now;
        updatedAt = now;

        if (status == null) {
            status = ClassStatus.OPEN;
        }
    }

    @PreUpdate
    public void preUpdate() {
        updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

package com.utefit.entity;

import com.utefit.enums.MembershipStatus;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Table(name = "memberships")
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class Membership {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "user_id",
            nullable = false,
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_membership_user")
    )
    private User user;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "branch_id",
            nullable = false,
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_membership_branch")
    )
    private Branch branch;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "plan_id",
            nullable = false,
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_membership_plan")
    )
    private MembershipPlan plan;

    @Column(name = "start_date")
    private LocalDate startDate;

    @Column(name = "end_date")
    private LocalDate endDate;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private MembershipStatus status;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist
    public void prePersist() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

        createdAt = now;
        updatedAt = now;

        if (status == null) {
            status = MembershipStatus.PENDING;
        }
    }

    @PreUpdate
    public void preUpdate() {
        updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

package com.utefit.entity;

import com.utefit.enums.OtpType;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Table(name = "otp_codes")
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class OtpCode {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "user_id", nullable = false)
    private User user;

    @Column(name = "code", nullable = false, length = 100)
    private String code;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 30)
    private OtpType type;

    @Column(name = "expired_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime expiredAt;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private Boolean used;

    @Column(name = "used_at")
    private LocalDateTime usedAt;

    @Column(name = "attempt_count")
    private Integer attemptCount;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @PrePersist
    public void prePersist() {
        if (createdAt == null) {
            createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        }

        if (used == null) {
            used = false;
        }

        if (attemptCount == null) {
            attemptCount = 0;
        }
    }
}

package com.utefit.entity;

import com.utefit.enums.PaymentMethod;
import com.utefit.enums.PaymentStatus;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Table(name = "payments")
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class Payment {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "user_id",
            nullable = false,
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_payment_user")
    )
    private User user;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "membership_id",
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_payment_membership")
    )
    private Membership membership;

    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 15, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal amount;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private PaymentMethod method;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private PaymentStatus status;

    @Column(name = "transaction_code", length = 100)
    private String transactionCode;

    @Column(name = "paid_at")
    private LocalDateTime paidAt;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @PrePersist
    public void prePersist() {
        if (createdAt == null) {
            createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        }

        if (status == null) {
            status = PaymentStatus.PENDING;
        }
    }
}

package com.utefit.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Table(
        name = "trainers",
        uniqueConstraints = {
                @UniqueConstraint(
                        name = "uk_trainers_user",
                        columnNames = "user_id"
                )
        }
)
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class Trainer {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @OneToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "user_id",
            nullable = false,
            unique = true,
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_trainer_user")
    )
    private User user;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(
            name = "branch_id",
            foreignKey = @ForeignKey(name = "fk_trainer_branch")
    )
    private Branch branch;

    @Column(length = 255)
    private String specialization;

    @Column(name = "years_experience")
    private Integer yearsExperience;

    @Column(length = 2000)
    private String bio;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private Boolean active;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist
    public void prePersist() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

        createdAt = now;
        updatedAt = now;

        if (active == null) {
            active = true;
        }
    }

    @PreUpdate
    public void preUpdate() {
        updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

package com.utefit.entity;

import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Table(name = "trainer_requests")
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class TrainerRequest {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    /**
     * Member gửi yêu cầu (User role = MEMBER)
     */
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "member_id", nullable = false)
    private User member;

    /**
     * Trainer nhận yêu cầu (entity Trainer)
     */
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "trainer_id", nullable = false)
    private Trainer trainer;

    @Column(name = "message", length = 2000)
    private String message;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "status", length = 20, nullable = false)
    private TrainerRequestStatus status;

    @Column(name = "requested_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime requestedAt;

    @Column(name = "responded_at")

}

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\entity\User.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.entity;

import com.utefit.enums.UserRole;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Table(name = "users")
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class User {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "first_name", nullable = false, length = 50)
    private String firstName;

    @Column(name = "last_name", nullable = false, length = 50)
    private String lastName;

    @Column(nullable = false, unique = true, length = 100)
    private String email;

    @Column(length = 20)
    private String phone;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private String password;

    @Column(name = "avatar_url")
    private String avatarUrl;

    @Column(name = "avatar_public_id")
    private String avatarPublicId;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private UserRole role;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private UserStatus status;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private Boolean enabled;

    @Column(name = "token_version")
    private Integer tokenVersion;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist
    public void prePersist() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

        if (createdAt == null) {
            createdAt = now;
        }

        updatedAt = now;

        if (role == null) {
            role = UserRole.MEMBER;
        }

        if (status == null) {
            status = UserStatus.UNVERIFIED;
        }

        if (enabled == null) {
            enabled = false;
        }

        if (tokenVersion == null) {
            tokenVersion = 0;
        }
    }

    @PreUpdate
    public void preUpdate() {
        updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

package com.utefit.repository;

import com.utefit.entity.Branch;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

import java.util.List;

public interface BranchRepository extends JpaRepository<Branch, Long> {

    /**
     * Dùng cho dropdown lọc PT ở trang /trainers (của B)
     * và các dropdown chọn chi nhánh đang hoạt động.
     */
    List<Branch> findByActiveTrueOrderByNameAsc();

    /**
     * Dùng cho trang quản lý chi nhánh /admin/branches (của C)
     */
    @Query("""
            SELECT b FROM Branch b
            WHERE (:keyword IS NULL
                   OR LOWER(b.name) LIKE :keyword
                   OR LOWER(b.address) LIKE :keyword
                   OR LOWER(b.phone) LIKE :keyword)
              AND (:active IS NULL OR b.active = :active)
            """)
    Page<Branch> searchBranches(
            @Param("keyword") String keyword,
            @Param("active") Boolean active,
            Pageable pageable
    );

    boolean existsByNameIgnoreCase(String name);

    boolean existsByNameIgnoreCaseAndIdNot(String name, Long id);
}

package com.utefit.repository;

import com.utefit.entity.ClassCategory;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;

import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

public interface ClassCategoryRepository extends JpaRepository<ClassCategory, Long> {

    boolean existsByNameIgnoreCase(String name);

    boolean existsByNameIgnoreCaseAndIdNot(String name, Long id);

    @Query("""
            SELECT c FROM ClassCategory c
            WHERE (:keyword IS NULL
                   OR LOWER(c.name) LIKE :keyword
                   OR LOWER(c.description) LIKE :keyword)
              AND (:active IS NULL OR c.active = :active)
            """)
    Page<ClassCategory> searchCategories(
            @Param("keyword") String keyword,
            @Param("active") Boolean active,
            Pageable pageable
    );
}

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\repository\ClassScheduleRepository.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.repository;

import com.utefit.entity.ClassSchedule;
import com.utefit.enums.ClassStatus;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.EntityGraph;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

public interface ClassScheduleRepository extends JpaRepository<ClassSchedule, Long> {

    @EntityGraph(attributePaths = {
            "fitnessClass",
            "fitnessClass.branch",
            "fitnessClass.category",
            "fitnessClass.trainer",
            "fitnessClass.trainer.user"
    })
    @Query(
            value = """
                    SELECT s FROM ClassSchedule s
                    JOIN s.fitnessClass fc
                    JOIN fc.branch b
                    LEFT JOIN fc.trainer t
                    WHERE (:fitnessClassId IS NULL OR fc.id = :fitnessClassId)
                      AND (:branchId IS NULL OR b.id = :branchId)
                      AND (:scheduleDate IS NULL OR s.scheduleDate = :scheduleDate)
                      AND (:active IS NULL OR s.active = :active)
                    """,
            countQuery = """
                    SELECT COUNT(s) FROM ClassSchedule s
                    JOIN s.fitnessClass fc
                    JOIN fc.branch b
                    WHERE (:fitnessClassId IS NULL OR fc.id = :fitnessClassId)
                      AND (:branchId IS NULL OR b.id = :branchId)
                      AND (:scheduleDate IS NULL OR s.scheduleDate = :scheduleDate)
                      AND (:active IS NULL OR s.active = :active)
                    """
    )
    Page<ClassSchedule> searchAdminSchedules(
            @Param("fitnessClassId") Long fitnessClassId,
            @Param("branchId") Long branchId,
            @Param("scheduleDate") LocalDate scheduleDate,
            @Param("active") Boolean active,
            Pageable pageable
    );

    @EntityGraph(attributePaths = {
            "fitnessClass",
            "fitnessClass.branch",
            "fitnessClass.category",
            "fitnessClass.trainer",
            "fitnessClass.trainer.user"
    })
    @Query("SELECT s FROM ClassSchedule s WHERE s.id = :id")
    Optional<ClassSchedule> findDetailById(@Param("id") Long id);

    // Ép kiểu CAST(... AS time) để tương thích tuyệt đối với kiểu dữ liệu time của SQL Server
    @EntityGraph(attributePaths = {"fitnessClass", "fitnessClass.branch"})
    @Query("""
            SELECT s FROM ClassSchedule s
            JOIN s.fitnessClass fc
            JOIN fc.trainer t
            WHERE t.id = :trainerId
              AND s.scheduleDate = :scheduleDate
              AND s.active = true
              AND fc.status <> :cancelledStatus
              AND s.id <> :excludeScheduleId
              AND NOT (s.endTime <= CAST(:startTime AS time) OR s.startTime >= CAST(:endTime AS time))
            """)
    List<ClassSchedule> findOverlappingTrainerSchedules(
            @Param("trainerId") Long trainerId,
            @Param("scheduleDate") LocalDate scheduleDate,
            @Param("startTime") LocalTime startTime,
            @Param("endTime") LocalTime endTime,
            @Param("cancelledStatus") ClassStatus cancelledStatus,
            @Param("excludeScheduleId") Long excludeScheduleId
    );

    @EntityGraph(attributePaths = {"fitnessClass"})
    @Query("""
            SELECT s FROM ClassSchedule s
            JOIN s.fitnessClass fc
            JOIN fc.branch b
            WHERE b.id = :branchId
              AND s.scheduleDate = :scheduleDate
              AND s.active = true
              AND fc.status <> :cancelledStatus
              AND s.room IS NOT NULL
              AND LOWER(s.room) = LOWER(:room)
              AND s.id <> :excludeScheduleId
              AND NOT (s.endTime <= CAST(:startTime AS time) OR s.startTime >= CAST(:endTime AS time))
            """)
    List<ClassSchedule> findOverlappingRoomSchedules(
            @Param("branchId") Long branchId,
            @Param("scheduleDate") LocalDate scheduleDate,
            @Param("startTime") LocalTime startTime,
            @Param("endTime") LocalTime endTime,
            @Param("room") String room,
            @Param("cancelledStatus") ClassStatus cancelledStatus,
            @Param("excludeScheduleId") Long excludeScheduleId
    );

    List<ClassSchedule> findByFitnessClassIdAndActiveTrue(Long fitnessClassId);
}

package com.utefit.repository;

import com.utefit.entity.FitnessClass;
import com.utefit.enums.ClassStatus;

public interface FitnessClassRepository extends JpaRepository<FitnessClass, Long> {

    @EntityGraph(attributePaths = {"branch", "category", "trainer", "trainer.user"})
    @Query(
            value = """
                    SELECT fc FROM FitnessClass fc
                    JOIN fc.branch b
                    JOIN fc.category c
                    LEFT JOIN fc.trainer t
                    LEFT JOIN t.user u
                    WHERE (:keyword IS NULL
                           OR LOWER(fc.name) LIKE :keyword
                           OR LOWER(fc.description) LIKE :keyword)
                      AND (:branchId IS NULL OR b.id = :branchId)
                      AND (:categoryId IS NULL OR c.id = :categoryId)
                      AND (:trainerId IS NULL OR t.id = :trainerId)
                      AND (:status IS NULL OR fc.status = :status)
                    """,
            countQuery = """
                    SELECT COUNT(fc) FROM FitnessClass fc
                    JOIN fc.branch b
                    JOIN fc.category c
                    LEFT JOIN fc.trainer t
                    WHERE (:keyword IS NULL
                           OR LOWER(fc.name) LIKE :keyword
                           OR LOWER(fc.description) LIKE :keyword)
                      AND (:branchId IS NULL OR b.id = :branchId)
                      AND (:categoryId IS NULL OR c.id = :categoryId)
                      AND (:trainerId IS NULL OR t.id = :trainerId)
                      AND (:status IS NULL OR fc.status = :status)
                    """
    )

package com.utefit.repository;

import com.utefit.entity.Trainer;
import com.utefit.enums.UserRole;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.EntityGraph;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

import java.util.Optional;

public interface TrainerRepository extends JpaRepository<Trainer, Long> {

    @Query("""
            SELECT t FROM Trainer t
            JOIN FETCH t.user u
            LEFT JOIN FETCH t.branch b
            WHERE t.id = :id
              AND t.active = true
              AND u.enabled = true
              AND u.status = :status
              AND u.role = :role
              AND (b IS NULL OR b.active = true)
            """)
    Optional<Trainer> findPublicTrainerById(
            @Param("id") Long id,
            @Param("status") UserStatus status,
            @Param("role") UserRole role
    );

    @EntityGraph(attributePaths = {"user", "branch"})
    @Query(
            value = """
                    SELECT t FROM Trainer t
                    JOIN t.user u
                    LEFT JOIN t.branch b
                    WHERE t.active = true
                      AND u.enabled = true
                      AND u.status = :status
                      AND u.role = :role
                      AND (b IS NULL OR b.active = true)
                      AND (
                          :keyword IS NULL
                          OR LOWER(u.firstName) LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', :keyword, '%'))
                          OR LOWER(u.lastName) LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', :keyword, '%'))
                          OR LOWER(CONCAT(u.firstName, ' ', u.lastName))
                             LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', :keyword, '%'))
                          OR LOWER(t.specialization)
                             LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', :keyword, '%'))
                      )
                      AND (:branchId IS NULL OR b.id = :branchId)
                    """,
            countQuery = """
                    SELECT COUNT(t) FROM Trainer t
                    JOIN t.user u
                    LEFT JOIN t.branch b
                    WHERE t.active = true
                      AND u.enabled = true
                      AND u.status = :status
                      AND u.role = :role
                      AND (b IS NULL OR b.active = true)
                      AND (
                          :keyword IS NULL
                          OR LOWER(u.firstName) LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', :keyword, '%'))
                          OR LOWER(u.lastName) LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', :keyword, '%'))
                          OR LOWER(CONCAT(u.firstName, ' ', u.lastName))
                             LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', :keyword, '%'))
                          OR LOWER(t.specialization)
                             LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', :keyword, '%'))
                      )
                      AND (:branchId IS NULL OR b.id = :branchId)
                    """
    )
    Page<Trainer> searchPublicTrainers(
            @Param("keyword") String keyword,
            @Param("branchId") Long branchId,
            @Param("status") UserStatus status,
            @Param("role") UserRole role,
            Pageable pageable
    );

    // ==================== BỔ SUNG HÀM 1 THAM SỐ CHO PT PROFILE ====================
    @Query("""
            SELECT t FROM Trainer t
            JOIN FETCH t.user u
            LEFT JOIN FETCH t.branch
            WHERE u.id = :userId
            """)
    Optional<Trainer> findByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);
    // ============================================================================

    @Query("""
            SELECT t FROM Trainer t
            JOIN FETCH t.user u
            LEFT JOIN FETCH t.branch
            WHERE u.id = :userId
              AND u.role = :role
            """)
    Optional<Trainer> findByUserId(
            @Param("userId") Long userId,
            @Param("role") UserRole role
    );

    // ==================== BỔ SUNG CHO PHÂN HỆ ADMIN (GIAI ĐOẠN 3 - THÀNH VIÊN C) ====================

    @EntityGraph(attributePaths = {"user", "branch"})
    @Query(
            value = """
                    SELECT t FROM Trainer t
                    JOIN t.user u
                    LEFT JOIN t.branch b
                    WHERE (
                          :keyword IS NULL
                          OR LOWER(u.firstName) LIKE :keyword
                          OR LOWER(u.lastName) LIKE :keyword
                          OR LOWER(CONCAT(u.firstName, ' ', u.lastName)) LIKE :keyword
                          OR LOWER(u.email) LIKE :keyword
                          OR LOWER(t.specialization) LIKE :keyword
                    )
                    AND (:branchId IS NULL OR b.id = :branchId)
                    AND (:active IS NULL OR t.active = :active)
                    """,
            countQuery = """
                    SELECT COUNT(t) FROM Trainer t

                          :keyword IS NULL
                          OR LOWER(u.firstName) LIKE :keyword
                          OR LOWER(u.lastName) LIKE :keyword
                          OR LOWER(CONCAT(u.firstName, ' ', u.lastName)) LIKE :keyword
                          OR LOWER(u.email) LIKE :keyword
                          OR LOWER(t.specialization) LIKE :keyword
                    )
                    AND (:branchId IS NULL OR b.id = :branchId)
                    AND (:active IS NULL OR t.active = :active)
                    """
    )
    Page<Trainer> searchAdminTrainers(
            @Param("keyword") String keyword,

            Pageable pageable
    );

    @Query("""

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\security\CustomUserDetailsService.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.security;

import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;

import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.*;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.util.Locale;

@Service
public class CustomUserDetailsService
        implements UserDetailsService {

    private final UserRepository users;

    public CustomUserDetailsService(UserRepository users) {
        this.users = users;
    }

    @Override
    public UserDetails loadUserByUsername(String email)
            throws UsernameNotFoundException {

        String normalized =
                email.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);

        User user = users.findByEmail(normalized)
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new UsernameNotFoundException(
                                "Không tìm thấy tài khoản"
                        )
                );

        return org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.User
                .withUsername(user.getEmail())
                .password(user.getPassword())
                .roles(user.getRole().name())
                .disabled(
                        !Boolean.TRUE.equals(user.getEnabled())
                                || user.getStatus() != UserStatus.ACTIVE
                )
                .accountLocked(
                        user.getStatus() == UserStatus.LOCKED
                )
                .build();
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\security\JwtAuthenticationFilter.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.security;

import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;

import io.jsonwebtoken.Claims;
import io.jsonwebtoken.JwtException;

import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.Cookie;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import org.springframework.security.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UserDetails;
import org.springframework.web.filter.OncePerRequestFilter;

import java.io.IOException;

public class JwtAuthenticationFilter
        extends OncePerRequestFilter {

    private final JwtService jwt;
    private final UserRepository users;
    private final CustomUserDetailsService detailsService;

    public JwtAuthenticationFilter(
            JwtService jwt,
            UserRepository users,
            CustomUserDetailsService detailsService
    ) {
        this.jwt = jwt;
        this.users = users;
        this.detailsService = detailsService;
    }

    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            HttpServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws ServletException, IOException {

        String token = null;

        // Lấy JWT từ cookie HttpOnly.
        if (request.getCookies() != null) {
            for (Cookie cookie : request.getCookies()) {

                if ("UTEFIT_AUTH".equals(cookie.getName())) {
                    token = cookie.getValue();
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if (token != null
                && SecurityContextHolder
                .getContext()
                .getAuthentication() == null) {

            try {
                Claims claims = jwt.parseVerified(token);

                Long id = Long.parseLong(
                        claims.getSubject()
                );

                User user = users.findById(id).orElse(null);

                Integer tokenVersion =
                        claims.get("ver", Integer.class);

                if (user != null
                        && user.getStatus() == UserStatus.ACTIVE
                        && Boolean.TRUE.equals(user.getEnabled())
                        && tokenVersion != null
                        && tokenVersion.equals(
                        user.getTokenVersion() == null
                                ? 0
                                : user.getTokenVersion()
                )) {

                    UserDetails principal =
                            detailsService.loadUserByUsername(
                                    user.getEmail()
                            );

                    var authentication =
                            new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
                                    principal,
                                    null,
                                    principal.getAuthorities()
                            );

                    SecurityContextHolder
                            .getContext()
                            .setAuthentication(authentication);
                }

            } catch (
                    JwtException
                    | IllegalArgumentException
                    | org.springframework.security.core.userdetails
                            .UsernameNotFoundException ignored
            ) {
                // Token không hợp lệ, xem như Guest.
            }
        }

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\security\JwtService.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.security;

import com.utefit.entity.User;

import io.jsonwebtoken.Claims;
import io.jsonwebtoken.Jwts;
import io.jsonwebtoken.io.Decoders;
import io.jsonwebtoken.security.Keys;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import javax.crypto.SecretKey;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Date;

@Service
public class JwtService {

    private final SecretKey key;

    public JwtService(
            @Value("${utefit.jwt.secret-base64}")
            String base64Secret
    ) {
        byte[] decoded =
                Decoders.BASE64.decode(base64Secret);

        if (decoded.length < 32) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "JWT secret phải có tối thiểu 32 bytes"
            );
        }

        this.key = Keys.hmacShaKeyFor(decoded);
    }

    public String generate(User user) {

        Instant now = Instant.now();

        int version = user.getTokenVersion() == null
                ? 0
                : user.getTokenVersion();

        return Jwts.builder()
                .issuer("UTEFit")
                .subject(user.getId().toString())
                .claim("ver", version)
                .issuedAt(Date.from(now))
                .expiration(
                        Date.from(
                                now.plus(Duration.ofHours(2))
                        )
                )
                .signWith(key, Jwts.SIG.HS256)
                .compact();
    }

    public Claims parseVerified(String token) {

        return Jwts.parser()
                .verifyWith(key)
                .requireIssuer("UTEFit")
                .build()
                .parseSignedClaims(token)
                .getPayload();
    }
}

package com.utefit.service;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.BranchFormDto;
import com.utefit.entity.Branch;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\service\impl\AdminBranchServiceImpl.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.BranchFormDto;
import com.utefit.dto.cloudinary.MediaUploadResult;
import com.utefit.entity.Branch;
import com.utefit.repository.BranchRepository;
import com.utefit.service.AdminBranchService;
import com.utefit.service.CloudinaryService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminBranchServiceImpl implements AdminBranchService {

    private final BranchRepository branchRepository;
    private final CloudinaryService cloudinaryService;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<Branch> getBranches(String keyword, Boolean active, int page, int size) {
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size, Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "createdAt"));
        String searchPattern = (keyword != null && !keyword.trim().isEmpty())
                ? "%" + keyword.trim().toLowerCase() + "%"
                : null;
        return branchRepository.searchBranches(searchPattern, active, pageable);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Branch getBranchById(Long id) {
        return branchRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy chi nhánh với ID: " + id));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public BranchFormDto getBranchFormById(Long id) {
        Branch b = getBranchById(id);
        return BranchFormDto.builder()
                .id(b.getId())
                .name(b.getName())
                .address(b.getAddress())
                .phone(b.getPhone())
                .description(b.getDescription())
                .imageUrl(b.getImageUrl())
                .imagePublicId(b.getImagePublicId())
                .openingTime(b.getOpeningTime())
                .closingTime(b.getClosingTime())
                .active(b.getActive())
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void createBranch(BranchFormDto dto) {
        validateBranchBusinessRules(dto, null);

        String imageUrl = dto.getImageUrl();
        String imagePublicId = dto.getImagePublicId();

        // Nếu người dùng có chọn file ảnh tải lên thì gọi CloudinaryService của B
        if (dto.getImageFile() != null && !dto.getImageFile().isEmpty()) {
            MediaUploadResult uploadResult = cloudinaryService.uploadImage(dto.getImageFile(), "utefit/branches");
            imageUrl = uploadResult.url();
            imagePublicId = uploadResult.publicId();
        }

        Branch branch = Branch.builder()
                .name(dto.getName().trim())
                .address(dto.getAddress().trim())
                .phone(dto.getPhone() != null ? dto.getPhone().trim() : null)
                .description(dto.getDescription())
                .imageUrl(imageUrl)
                .imagePublicId(imagePublicId)
                .openingTime(dto.getOpeningTime())
                .closingTime(dto.getClosingTime())
                .active(dto.getActive() != null ? dto.getActive() : true)
                .build();

        branchRepository.save(branch);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updateBranch(Long id, BranchFormDto dto) {
        Branch branch = getBranchById(id);
        validateBranchBusinessRules(dto, id);

        branch.setName(dto.getName().trim());
        branch.setAddress(dto.getAddress().trim());
        branch.setPhone(dto.getPhone() != null ? dto.getPhone().trim() : null);
        branch.setDescription(dto.getDescription());
        branch.setOpeningTime(dto.getOpeningTime());
        branch.setClosingTime(dto.getClosingTime());
        branch.setActive(dto.getActive() != null ? dto.getActive() : true);

        // Nếu người dùng tải lên file ảnh mới -> Xóa ảnh cũ trên Cloudinary (nếu có) rồi upload ảnh mới
        if (dto.getImageFile() != null && !dto.getImageFile().isEmpty()) {
            if (branch.getImagePublicId() != null && !branch.getImagePublicId().isBlank()) {
                cloudinaryService.delete(branch.getImagePublicId());
            }
            MediaUploadResult uploadResult = cloudinaryService.uploadImage(dto.getImageFile(), "utefit/branches");
            branch.setImageUrl(uploadResult.url());
            branch.setImagePublicId(uploadResult.publicId());
        } else if (dto.getImageUrl() != null && !dto.getImageUrl().isBlank()) {
            branch.setImageUrl(dto.getImageUrl().trim());
        }

        branchRepository.save(branch);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void toggleActive(Long id) {
        Branch branch = getBranchById(id);
        branch.setActive(!Boolean.TRUE.equals(branch.getActive()));
        branchRepository.save(branch);
    }

    // Business Rule trọng tâm để chụp Source Evidence cho báo cáo
    private void validateBranchBusinessRules(BranchFormDto dto, Long currentId) {
        if (dto.getOpeningTime() != null && dto.getClosingTime() != null) {
            if (!dto.getOpeningTime().isBefore(dto.getClosingTime())) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Giờ mở cửa phải nhỏ hơn giờ đóng cửa!");
            }
        }

        String name = dto.getName().trim();
        boolean duplicateName = (currentId == null)
                ? branchRepository.existsByNameIgnoreCase(name)
                : branchRepository.existsByNameIgnoreCaseAndIdNot(name, currentId);

        if (duplicateName) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Tên chi nhánh '" + name + "' đã tồn tại trong hệ thống!");
        }
    }
}

package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.ClassCategoryFormDto;
import com.utefit.entity.ClassCategory;
import com.utefit.repository.ClassCategoryRepository;
import com.utefit.service.AdminCategoryService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminCategoryServiceImpl implements AdminCategoryService {

    private final ClassCategoryRepository categoryRepository;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<ClassCategory> getCategories(String keyword, Boolean active, int page, int size) {
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size, Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "createdAt"));
        String searchPattern = (keyword != null && !keyword.trim().isEmpty())
                ? "%" + keyword.trim().toLowerCase() + "%"
                : null;
        return categoryRepository.searchCategories(searchPattern, active, pageable);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public ClassCategory getCategoryById(Long id) {
        return categoryRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy loại lớp với ID: " + id));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public ClassCategoryFormDto getCategoryFormById(Long id) {
        ClassCategory c = getCategoryById(id);
        return ClassCategoryFormDto.builder()
                .id(c.getId())
                .name(c.getName())
                .description(c.getDescription())
                .active(c.getActive())
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void createCategory(ClassCategoryFormDto dto) {
        validateUniqueCategoryName(dto.getName(), null);

        ClassCategory category = ClassCategory.builder()
                .name(dto.getName().trim())
                .description(dto.getDescription() != null ? dto.getDescription().trim() : null)
                .active(dto.getActive() != null ? dto.getActive() : true)
                .build();

        categoryRepository.save(category);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updateCategory(Long id, ClassCategoryFormDto dto) {
        ClassCategory category = getCategoryById(id);
        validateUniqueCategoryName(dto.getName(), id);

        category.setName(dto.getName().trim());
        category.setDescription(dto.getDescription() != null ? dto.getDescription().trim() : null);
        category.setActive(dto.getActive() != null ? dto.getActive() : true);

        categoryRepository.save(category);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void toggleActive(Long id) {
        ClassCategory category = getCategoryById(id);
        category.setActive(!Boolean.TRUE.equals(category.getActive()));
        categoryRepository.save(category);
    }

    private void validateUniqueCategoryName(String rawName, Long currentId) {
        String name = rawName.trim();
        boolean duplicate = (currentId == null)
                ? categoryRepository.existsByNameIgnoreCase(name)
                : categoryRepository.existsByNameIgnoreCaseAndIdNot(name, currentId);

        if (duplicate) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Tên loại lớp '" + name + "' đã tồn tại trong hệ thống!");
        }
    }
}

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\service\impl\AdminClassServiceImpl.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.FitnessClassFormDto;
import com.utefit.entity.*;
import com.utefit.enums.ClassStatus;
import com.utefit.enums.UserRole;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import com.utefit.repository.*;
import com.utefit.service.AdminClassService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminClassServiceImpl implements AdminClassService {

    private final FitnessClassRepository fitnessClassRepository;
    private final ClassScheduleRepository classScheduleRepository;
    private final BranchRepository branchRepository;
    private final ClassCategoryRepository categoryRepository;
    private final TrainerRepository trainerRepository;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<FitnessClass> getClasses(
            String keyword,
            Long branchId,
            Long categoryId,
            Long trainerId,
            ClassStatus status,
            int page,
            int size
    ) {
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size, Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "id"));
        String searchPattern = (keyword != null && !keyword.trim().isEmpty())
                ? "%" + keyword.trim().toLowerCase() + "%"
                : null;
        return fitnessClassRepository.searchAdminClasses(
                searchPattern, branchId, categoryId, trainerId, status, pageable
        );
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public FitnessClass getClassById(Long id) {
        return fitnessClassRepository.findDetailById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy lớp tập với ID: " + id));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public FitnessClassFormDto getClassFormById(Long id) {
        FitnessClass fc = getClassById(id);
        return FitnessClassFormDto.builder()
                .id(fc.getId())
                .name(fc.getName())
                .description(fc.getDescription())
                .branchId(fc.getBranch() != null ? fc.getBranch().getId() : null)
                .categoryId(fc.getCategory() != null ? fc.getCategory().getId() : null)
                .trainerId(fc.getTrainer() != null ? fc.getTrainer().getId() : null)
                .capacity(fc.getCapacity())
                .status(fc.getStatus())
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void createClass(FitnessClassFormDto dto) {
        validateCapacity(dto.getCapacity());

        Branch branch = requireActiveBranch(dto.getBranchId());
        ClassCategory category = requireActiveCategory(dto.getCategoryId());
        Trainer trainer = resolveAndValidateTrainer(dto.getTrainerId(), branch, category);

        FitnessClass fitnessClass = FitnessClass.builder()
                .name(dto.getName().trim())
                .description(dto.getDescription() != null ? dto.getDescription().trim() : null)
                .branch(branch)
                .category(category)
                .trainer(trainer)
                .capacity(dto.getCapacity())
                .status(dto.getStatus() != null ? dto.getStatus() : ClassStatus.OPEN)
                .build();

        fitnessClassRepository.save(fitnessClass);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updateClass(Long id, FitnessClassFormDto dto) {
        FitnessClass fitnessClass = getClassById(id);
        validateCapacity(dto.getCapacity());

        Branch branch = requireActiveBranch(dto.getBranchId());
        ClassCategory category = requireActiveCategory(dto.getCategoryId());
        Trainer trainer = resolveAndValidateTrainer(dto.getTrainerId(), branch, category);

        ClassStatus targetStatus = dto.getStatus() != null ? dto.getStatus() : fitnessClass.getStatus();

        fitnessClass.setName(dto.getName().trim());
        fitnessClass.setDescription(dto.getDescription() != null ? dto.getDescription().trim() : null);
        fitnessClass.setBranch(branch);
        fitnessClass.setCategory(category);
        fitnessClass.setTrainer(trainer);
        fitnessClass.setCapacity(dto.getCapacity());
        fitnessClass.setStatus(targetStatus);

        fitnessClassRepository.save(fitnessClass);

        if (targetStatus == ClassStatus.CANCELLED) {
            deactivateSchedulesOfClass(id);
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void cancelClass(Long id) {
        FitnessClass fitnessClass = getClassById(id);
        if (fitnessClass.getStatus() == ClassStatus.CANCELLED) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Lớp tập này đã ở trạng thái HỦY trước đó!");
        }

        fitnessClass.setStatus(ClassStatus.CANCELLED);
        fitnessClassRepository.save(fitnessClass);

        deactivateSchedulesOfClass(id);
    }

    private void deactivateSchedulesOfClass(Long classId) {
        List<ClassSchedule> activeSchedules = classScheduleRepository.findByFitnessClassIdAndActiveTrue(classId);
        for (ClassSchedule schedule : activeSchedules) {
            schedule.setActive(false);
        }
        if (!activeSchedules.isEmpty()) {
            classScheduleRepository.saveAll(activeSchedules);
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<Branch> getActiveBranches() {
        return branchRepository.findAll(Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "name"))
                .stream()
                .filter(b -> Boolean.TRUE.equals(b.getActive()))
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<ClassCategory> getActiveCategories() {
        return categoryRepository.findAll(Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "name"))
                .stream()
                .filter(c -> Boolean.TRUE.equals(c.getActive()))
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<Trainer> getActiveTrainers() {
        return trainerRepository.searchAdminTrainers(null, null, true, Pageable.unpaged())
                .getContent()
                .stream()
                .filter(t -> Boolean.TRUE.equals(t.getActive())
                        && t.getUser() != null
                        && Boolean.TRUE.equals(t.getUser().getEnabled())
                        && t.getUser().getStatus() == UserStatus.ACTIVE
                        && t.getUser().getRole() == UserRole.PT)
                .toList();
    }

    private void validateCapacity(Integer capacity) {
        if (capacity == null || capacity <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Sức chứa của lớp tập (Capacity) phải lớn hơn 0!");
        }
    }

    private Branch requireActiveBranch(Long branchId) {
        Branch branch = branchRepository.findById(branchId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy chi nhánh với ID: " + branchId));
        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(branch.getActive())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Chi nhánh '" + branch.getName() + "' đang tạm ngưng hoạt động, không thể gán lớp tập!");
        }
        return branch;
    }

    private ClassCategory requireActiveCategory(Long categoryId) {
        ClassCategory category = categoryRepository.findById(categoryId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy loại lớp với ID: " + categoryId));
        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(category.getActive())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Loại lớp '" + category.getName() + "' đang tạm ngưng hoạt động!");
        }
        return category;
    }

    private Trainer resolveAndValidateTrainer(Long trainerId, Branch classBranch, ClassCategory category) {
        if (trainerId == null) {
            return null;
        }
        Trainer trainer = trainerRepository.findAdminTrainerById(trainerId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy huấn luyện viên với ID: " + trainerId));

        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(trainer.getActive())
                || trainer.getUser() == null
                || !Boolean.TRUE.equals(trainer.getUser().getEnabled())
                || trainer.getUser().getStatus() != UserStatus.ACTIVE) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Huấn luyện viên được chọn hiện không ở trạng thái ACTIVE!");
        }

        if (trainer.getBranch() != null && !trainer.getBranch().getId().equals(classBranch.getId())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "PT '" + trainer.getUser().getFirstName() + " " + trainer.getUser().getLastName()
                            + "' đang thuộc chi nhánh '" + trainer.getBranch().getName()
                            + "', không khớp với chi nhánh '" + classBranch.getName() + "' của lớp tập!"
            );
        }

        String spec = trainer.getSpecialization() != null ? trainer.getSpecialization().trim().toLowerCase() : "";
        String catName = category.getName() != null ? category.getName().trim().toLowerCase() : "";

        if (!spec.isEmpty()
                && !spec.contains("general")
                && !spec.contains("đa năng")
                && !spec.contains(catName)
                && !catName.contains(spec)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Chuyên môn của PT (" + trainer.getSpecialization()
                            + ") không phù hợp để dạy loại lớp '" + category.getName() + "'!"
            );
        }

        return trainer;
    }
}

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\service\impl\AdminDashboardServiceImpl.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.repository.BranchRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.FitnessClassRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.TrainerRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;
import com.utefit.service.AdminDashboardService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminDashboardServiceImpl implements AdminDashboardService {

    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final BranchRepository branchRepository;
    private final TrainerRepository trainerRepository;
    private final FitnessClassRepository fitnessClassRepository;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Map<String, Long> getBasicKpiCounts() {
        Map<String, Long> kpis = new HashMap<>();
        kpis.put("totalUsers", userRepository.count());
        kpis.put("totalBranches", branchRepository.count());
        kpis.put("totalTrainers", trainerRepository.count());
        kpis.put("totalClasses", fitnessClassRepository.count());
        return kpis;
    }
}

package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.MembershipPlanFormDto;
import com.utefit.entity.MembershipPlan;
import com.utefit.repository.MembershipPlanRepository;
import com.utefit.service.AdminMembershipPlanService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminMembershipPlanServiceImpl implements AdminMembershipPlanService {

    private final MembershipPlanRepository planRepository;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<MembershipPlan> getPlans(String keyword, Boolean active, int page, int size) {
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size, Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "durationDays"));
        String searchPattern = (keyword != null && !keyword.trim().isEmpty())
                ? "%" + keyword.trim().toLowerCase() + "%"
                : null;
        return planRepository.searchPlans(searchPattern, active, pageable);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public MembershipPlan getPlanById(Long id) {
        return planRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy gói tập với ID: " + id));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public MembershipPlanFormDto getPlanFormById(Long id) {
        MembershipPlan p = getPlanById(id);
        return MembershipPlanFormDto.builder()
                .id(p.getId())
                .name(p.getName())
                .description(p.getDescription())
                .durationDays(p.getDurationDays())
                .price(p.getPrice())
                .active(p.getActive())
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void createPlan(MembershipPlanFormDto dto) {
        validatePlanBusinessRules(dto, null);

        MembershipPlan plan = MembershipPlan.builder()
                .name(dto.getName().trim())
                .description(dto.getDescription() != null ? dto.getDescription().trim() : null)
                .durationDays(dto.getDurationDays())
                .price(dto.getPrice())
                .active(dto.getActive() != null ? dto.getActive() : true)
                .build();

        planRepository.save(plan);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updatePlan(Long id, MembershipPlanFormDto dto) {
        MembershipPlan plan = getPlanById(id);
        validatePlanBusinessRules(dto, id);

        plan.setName(dto.getName().trim());
        plan.setDescription(dto.getDescription() != null ? dto.getDescription().trim() : null);
        plan.setDurationDays(dto.getDurationDays());
        plan.setPrice(dto.getPrice());
        plan.setActive(dto.getActive() != null ? dto.getActive() : true);

        planRepository.save(plan);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void toggleActive(Long id) {
        MembershipPlan plan = getPlanById(id);
        plan.setActive(!Boolean.TRUE.equals(plan.getActive()));
        planRepository.save(plan);
    }

    // Business Rules cốt lõi Giai đoạn 3: price > 0, duration > 0, chống trùng tên
    private void validatePlanBusinessRules(MembershipPlanFormDto dto, Long currentId) {
        if (dto.getPrice() == null || dto.getPrice().compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Giá gói tập phải lớn hơn 0!");
        }
        if (dto.getDurationDays() == null || dto.getDurationDays() <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Thời hạn gói tập (số ngày) phải lớn hơn 0!");
        }

        String name = dto.getName().trim();
        boolean duplicate = (currentId == null)
                ? planRepository.existsByNameIgnoreCase(name)
                : planRepository.existsByNameIgnoreCaseAndIdNot(name, currentId);

        if (duplicate) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Tên gói tập '" + name + "' đã tồn tại trong hệ thống!");
        }
    }
}
