package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.ClassScheduleFormDto;
import com.utefit.entity.Branch;
import com.utefit.entity.ClassSchedule;
import com.utefit.entity.FitnessClass;
import com.utefit.entity.Trainer;
import com.utefit.enums.ClassStatus;
import com.utefit.repository.BranchRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.ClassScheduleRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.FitnessClassRepository;
import com.utefit.service.AdminScheduleService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminScheduleServiceImpl implements AdminScheduleService {

    private final ClassScheduleRepository scheduleRepository;
    private final FitnessClassRepository fitnessClassRepository;
    private final BranchRepository branchRepository;

    private static final DateTimeFormatter DATE_FMT = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd/MM/yyyy");

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<ClassSchedule> getSchedules(
            Long fitnessClassId,
            Long branchId,
            LocalDate scheduleDate,
            Boolean active,
            int page,
            int size
    ) {
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(
                page,
                size,
                Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "scheduleDate")
                        .and(Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "startTime"))
        );
        return scheduleRepository.searchAdminSchedules(fitnessClassId, branchId, scheduleDate, active, pageable);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public ClassSchedule getScheduleById(Long id) {
        return scheduleRepository.findDetailById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy lịch lớp tập với ID: " + id));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public ClassScheduleFormDto getScheduleFormById(Long id) {
        ClassSchedule s = getScheduleById(id);
        return ClassScheduleFormDto.builder()
                .id(s.getId())
                .fitnessClassId(s.getFitnessClass().getId())
                .scheduleDate(s.getScheduleDate())
                .startTime(s.getStartTime())
                .endTime(s.getEndTime())
                .room(s.getRoom())
                .active(s.getActive())
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void createSchedule(ClassScheduleFormDto dto) {
        FitnessClass fitnessClass = validateScheduleBusinessRules(dto, null);

        ClassSchedule schedule = ClassSchedule.builder()
                .fitnessClass(fitnessClass)
                .scheduleDate(dto.getScheduleDate())
                .startTime(dto.getStartTime())
                .endTime(dto.getEndTime())
                .room(dto.getRoom() != null && !dto.getRoom().trim().isEmpty() ? dto.getRoom().trim() : null)
                .active(dto.getActive() != null ? dto.getActive() : true)
                .build();

        scheduleRepository.save(schedule);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void updateSchedule(Long id, ClassScheduleFormDto dto) {
        ClassSchedule schedule = getScheduleById(id);
        FitnessClass fitnessClass = validateScheduleBusinessRules(dto, id);

        schedule.setFitnessClass(fitnessClass);
        schedule.setScheduleDate(dto.getScheduleDate());
        schedule.setStartTime(dto.getStartTime());
        schedule.setEndTime(dto.getEndTime());
        schedule.setRoom(dto.getRoom() != null && !dto.getRoom().trim().isEmpty() ? dto.getRoom().trim() : null);
        schedule.setActive(dto.getActive() != null ? dto.getActive() : true);

        scheduleRepository.save(schedule);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void toggleActive(Long id) {
        ClassSchedule schedule = getScheduleById(id);
        boolean nextActive = !Boolean.TRUE.equals(schedule.getActive());

        // Nếu bật lại lịch học, kiểm tra lại điều kiện chống trùng lịch PT & phòng
        if (nextActive) {
            ClassScheduleFormDto checkDto = ClassScheduleFormDto.builder()
                    .id(schedule.getId())
                    .fitnessClassId(schedule.getFitnessClass().getId())
                    .scheduleDate(schedule.getScheduleDate())
                    .startTime(schedule.getStartTime())
                    .endTime(schedule.getEndTime())
                    .room(schedule.getRoom())
                    .active(true)
                    .build();
            validateScheduleBusinessRules(checkDto, schedule.getId());
        }

        schedule.setActive(nextActive);
        scheduleRepository.save(schedule);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<FitnessClass> getSchedulableClasses() {
        return fitnessClassRepository.findAllByStatusInOrderByNameAsc(
                List.of(ClassStatus.OPEN, ClassStatus.FULL)
        );
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<Branch> getAllBranches() {
        return branchRepository.findAll(Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "name"));
    }

    // ==================== BUSINESS RULES TRỌNG TÂM GIAI ĐOẠN 4 ====================
    private FitnessClass validateScheduleBusinessRules(ClassScheduleFormDto dto, Long currentScheduleId) {
        // 1. Kiểm tra startTime < endTime
        if (dto.getStartTime() == null || dto.getEndTime() == null || !dto.getStartTime().isBefore(dto.getEndTime())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Giờ bắt đầu phải sớm hơn giờ kết thúc (startTime < endTime)!");
        }

        // 2. Kiểm tra lớp tập hợp lệ và không bị CANCELLED / COMPLETED
        FitnessClass fitnessClass = fitnessClassRepository.findDetailById(dto.getFitnessClassId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy lớp tập đã chọn!"));

        if (fitnessClass.getStatus() == ClassStatus.CANCELLED || fitnessClass.getStatus() == ClassStatus.COMPLETED) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Không thể xếp lịch hoặc kích hoạt lịch cho lớp tập đã HỦY hoặc ĐÃ KẾT THÚC (" + fitnessClass.getName() + ")!"
            );
        }

        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(fitnessClass.getBranch().getActive())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Chi nhánh của lớp tập này hiện đang ngừng hoạt động!");
        }

        boolean isActiveSchedule = dto.getActive() == null || Boolean.TRUE.equals(dto.getActive());
        Long safeExcludeId = (currentScheduleId != null) ? currentScheduleId : -1L;

        // 3. Kiểm tra chống trùng lịch PT (Trainer Time Overlap) nếu lớp có gán PT và lịch đang active
        Trainer trainer = fitnessClass.getTrainer();
        if (isActiveSchedule && trainer != null) {
            if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(trainer.getActive())) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Huấn luyện viên phụ trách lớp này hiện đang ngừng hoạt động!");
            }

            List<ClassSchedule> trainerConflicts = scheduleRepository.findOverlappingTrainerSchedules(
                    trainer.getId(),
                    dto.getScheduleDate(),
                    dto.getStartTime(),
                    dto.getEndTime(),
                    ClassStatus.CANCELLED,
                    safeExcludeId
            );

            if (!trainerConflicts.isEmpty()) {
                ClassSchedule conflict = trainerConflicts.get(0);
                String ptName = trainer.getUser().getFirstName() + " " + trainer.getUser().getLastName();
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                        String.format(
                                "Trùng lịch PT! Huấn luyện viên %s đã có lịch dạy lớp '%s' từ %s đến %s ngày %s.",
                                ptName,
                                conflict.getFitnessClass().getName(),
                                conflict.getStartTime(),
                                conflict.getEndTime(),
                                conflict.getScheduleDate().format(DATE_FMT)
                        )
                );
            }
        }

        // 4. Kiểm tra chống trùng phòng tập (Room Overlap) trong cùng chi nhánh
        if (isActiveSchedule && dto.getRoom() != null && !dto.getRoom().trim().isEmpty()) {
            List<ClassSchedule> roomConflicts = scheduleRepository.findOverlappingRoomSchedules(
                    fitnessClass.getBranch().getId(),
                    dto.getScheduleDate(),
                    dto.getStartTime(),
                    dto.getEndTime(),
                    dto.getRoom().trim(),
                    ClassStatus.CANCELLED,
                    safeExcludeId
            );

            if (!roomConflicts.isEmpty()) {
                ClassSchedule conflict = roomConflicts.get(0);
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                        String.format(
                                "Trùng phòng tập! Phòng '%s' tại chi nhánh %s đã được xếp cho lớp '%s' (%s - %s) ngày %s.",
                                dto.getRoom().trim(),
                                fitnessClass.getBranch().getName(),
                                conflict.getFitnessClass().getName(),
                                conflict.getStartTime(),
                                conflict.getEndTime(),
                                conflict.getScheduleDate().format(DATE_FMT)
                        )
                );
            }
        }

        return fitnessClass;
    }
}

package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.entity.Branch;
import com.utefit.entity.Trainer;
import com.utefit.repository.BranchRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.TrainerRepository;
import com.utefit.service.AdminTrainerService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminTrainerServiceImpl implements AdminTrainerService {

    private final TrainerRepository trainerRepository;
    private final BranchRepository branchRepository;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<Trainer> getTrainers(String keyword, Long branchId, Boolean active, int page, int size) {
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size, Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "id"));
        String searchPattern = (keyword != null && !keyword.trim().isEmpty())
                ? "%" + keyword.trim().toLowerCase() + "%"
                : null;
        return trainerRepository.searchAdminTrainers(searchPattern, branchId, active, pageable);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Trainer getTrainerById(Long id) {
        return trainerRepository.findAdminTrainerById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy huấn luyện viên với ID: " + id));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<Branch> getAllBranches() {
        return branchRepository.findAll(Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "name"));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void assignBranch(Long trainerId, Long branchId) {
        Trainer trainer = getTrainerById(trainerId);

        if (branchId == null) {
            trainer.setBranch(null);
        } else {
            Branch branch = branchRepository.findById(branchId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy chi nhánh với ID: " + branchId));
            trainer.setBranch(branch);
        }

        trainerRepository.save(trainer);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void toggleActive(Long trainerId) {
        Trainer trainer = getTrainerById(trainerId);
        trainer.setActive(!Boolean.TRUE.equals(trainer.getActive()));
        trainerRepository.save(trainer);
    }
}

package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.dto.admin.AdminUserResponse;
import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.enums.UserRole;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;
import com.utefit.service.AdminUserService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminUserServiceImpl implements AdminUserService {

    private final UserRepository userRepository;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<AdminUserResponse> getUsers(String keyword, UserRole role, UserStatus status, int page, int size) {
        // Sắp xếp theo ID tăng dần để danh sách thẳng thớm, đúng thứ tự #1, #2, #3...
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size, Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "id"));

        String searchPattern = (keyword != null && !keyword.trim().isEmpty())
                ? "%" + keyword.trim().toLowerCase() + "%"
                : null;
        return userRepository.searchUsers(searchPattern, role, status, pageable)
                .map(AdminUserResponse::fromEntity);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public AdminUserResponse getUserById(Long id) {
        User user = userRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy người dùng với ID: " + id));
        return AdminUserResponse.fromEntity(user);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void toggleLockUser(Long userId, String currentAdminEmail) {
        User user = userRepository.findById(userId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Không tìm thấy người dùng với ID: " + userId));

        // Business Rule: Không cho phép Admin tự khóa tài khoản của chính mình
        if (currentAdminEmail != null && user.getEmail().equalsIgnoreCase(currentAdminEmail)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Bạn không thể tự khóa tài khoản Admin đang đăng nhập!");
        }

        if (user.getStatus() == UserStatus.LOCKED) {
            user.setStatus(UserStatus.ACTIVE);
            user.setEnabled(true);
        } else {
            user.setStatus(UserStatus.LOCKED);
            user.setEnabled(false);
        }

        userRepository.save(user);
    }
}

============================================================
package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.dto.auth.*;
import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.enums.*;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;
import com.utefit.service.AuthService;
import com.utefit.service.OtpService;
import com.utefit.exception.InvalidOtpException;

import org.springframework.security.authentication.AuthenticationManager;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.Locale;

@Service
public class AuthServiceImpl implements AuthService {

    private final UserRepository users;
    private final PasswordEncoder encoder;
    private final OtpService otpService;
    private final AuthenticationManager authenticationManager;

    public AuthServiceImpl(
            UserRepository users,
            PasswordEncoder encoder,
            OtpService otpService,
            AuthenticationManager authenticationManager
    ) {
        this.users = users;
        this.encoder = encoder;
        this.otpService = otpService;
        this.authenticationManager = authenticationManager;
    }

    private String normalize(String email) {
        return email.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public String register(RegisterRequest request) {

        if (!request.getPassword()
                .equals(request.getConfirmPassword())) {

            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Mật khẩu xác nhận không khớp."
            );
        }

        String email = normalize(request.getEmail());

        if (users.existsByEmail(email)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Email đã tồn tại."
            );
        }

        User user = new User();

        user.setFirstName(request.getFirstName().trim());
        user.setLastName(request.getLastName().trim());
        user.setEmail(email);
        user.setPhone(request.getPhone());

        // BCrypt
        user.setPassword(
                encoder.encode(request.getPassword())
        );

        // Guest chỉ được đăng ký MEMBER.
        user.setRole(UserRole.MEMBER);

        user.setStatus(UserStatus.UNVERIFIED);
        user.setEnabled(false);
        user.setTokenVersion(0);

        users.saveAndFlush(user);

        // Gửi OTP đăng ký.
        otpService.issueAndSend(
                user,
                OtpType.REGISTER
        );

        return email;
    }


    @Override
    @Transactional(
            noRollbackFor = InvalidOtpException.class
    )
    public void verifyRegistration(
            VerifyOtpRequest request
    ) {
        User user = users
                .findByEmail(normalize(request.getEmail()))
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Thông tin xác thực không hợp lệ."
                        )
                );

        if (user.getStatus() != UserStatus.UNVERIFIED) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Tài khoản đã xác thực hoặc bị khóa."
            );
        }

        boolean valid = otpService.consume(
                user,
                OtpType.REGISTER,
                request.getCode()
        );

        if (!valid) {
            throw new InvalidOtpException(
                    "OTP sai, hết hạn hoặc đã sử dụng."
            );
        }

        user.setStatus(UserStatus.ACTIVE);
        user.setEnabled(true);

        users.save(user);
    }


    @Override
    public void resendRegistrationOtp(String email) {

        User user = users
                .findByEmail(normalize(email))
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Không thể gửi OTP."
                        )
                );

        if (user.getStatus() != UserStatus.UNVERIFIED) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Tài khoản không chờ xác thực."
            );
        }

        otpService.issueAndSend(
                user,
                OtpType.REGISTER
        );
    }

    @Override
    public User authenticate(
            String email,
            String rawPassword
    ) {
        String normalized = normalize(email);

        authenticationManager.authenticate(
                new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
                        normalized,
                        rawPassword
                )
        );

        return users
                .findByEmail(normalized)
                .orElseThrow();
    }

    @Override
    public void requestPasswordReset(String email) {

        users.findByEmail(normalize(email))
                .ifPresent(user -> {

                    if (user.getStatus() == UserStatus.ACTIVE
                            && Boolean.TRUE.equals(
                            user.getEnabled())) {

                        otpService.issueAndSend(
                                user,
                                OtpType.FORGOT_PASSWORD
                        );
                    }
                });
    }


    @Override
    @Transactional(
            noRollbackFor = InvalidOtpException.class
    )
    public void resetPassword(
            ResetPasswordRequest request
    ) {
        if (!request.getNewPassword()
                .equals(request.getConfirmPassword())) {

            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Mật khẩu xác nhận không khớp."
            );
        }

        User user = users
                .findByEmail(normalize(request.getEmail()))
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "OTP không hợp lệ."
                        )
                );

        if (user.getStatus() != UserStatus.ACTIVE
                || !Boolean.TRUE.equals(user.getEnabled())) {

            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Tài khoản không được phép đổi mật khẩu."
            );
        }

        boolean valid = otpService.consume(
                user,
                OtpType.FORGOT_PASSWORD,
                request.getCode()
        );

        if (!valid) {
            throw new InvalidOtpException(
                    "OTP sai, hết hạn hoặc đã sử dụng."
            );
        }

        user.setPassword(
                encoder.encode(request.getNewPassword())
        );

        int oldVersion = user.getTokenVersion() == null
                ? 0
                : user.getTokenVersion();

        user.setTokenVersion(oldVersion + 1);

        users.save(user);
    }


    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void revokeTokensForUser(String email) {

        users.findByEmail(normalize(email))
                .ifPresent(user -> {

                    int oldVersion =
                            user.getTokenVersion() == null
                                    ? 0
                                    : user.getTokenVersion();

                    user.setTokenVersion(oldVersion + 1);
                });
    }
}

package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.cloudinary.Cloudinary;
import com.cloudinary.utils.ObjectUtils;
import com.utefit.dto.cloudinary.MediaUploadResult;
import com.utefit.service.CloudinaryService;
import com.utefit.validation.ImageValidator;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Map;

@Service
public class CloudinaryServiceImpl implements CloudinaryService {

    private final Cloudinary cloudinary;
    private final ImageValidator imageValidator;

    public CloudinaryServiceImpl(Cloudinary cloudinary,
                                 ImageValidator imageValidator) {
        this.cloudinary = cloudinary;
        this.imageValidator = imageValidator;
    }

    @Override
    public MediaUploadResult uploadImage(MultipartFile file, String folder) {

        if (file == null || file.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("File ảnh không được để trống");
        }

        // Kiểm tra định dạng, kích thước, MIME type...
        imageValidator.validate(file);

        try {
            Map<String, Object> uploadParams = ObjectUtils.asMap(
                    "folder", folder,
                    "resource_type", "image",
                    "overwrite", true
            );

            @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
            Map<String, Object> uploadResult = cloudinary.uploader()
                    .upload(file.getBytes(), uploadParams);

            String url = (String) uploadResult.get("secure_url");
            String publicId = (String) uploadResult.get("public_id");

            if (url == null || publicId == null) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("Upload ảnh lên Cloudinary thất bại");
            }

            return new MediaUploadResult(url, publicId);

        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Lỗi IO khi upload ảnh lên Cloudinary", e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void delete(String publicId) {

        if (publicId == null || publicId.isBlank()) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            Map<String, Object> params = ObjectUtils.asMap(
                    "invalidate", true
            );

            cloudinary.uploader().destroy(publicId, params);

        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Lỗi IO khi xoá ảnh trên Cloudinary", e);
        }
    }
}

package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.service.MailService;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.mail.SimpleMailMessage;
import org.springframework.mail.javamail.JavaMailSender;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
public class MailServiceImpl implements MailService {

    private final JavaMailSender sender;
    private final String from;

    public MailServiceImpl(
            JavaMailSender sender,
            @Value("${utefit.mail.from}") String from
    ) {
        this.sender = sender;
        this.from = from;
    }

    @Override
    public void sendOtp(
            String recipient,
            String code,
            String reason
    ) {
        SimpleMailMessage mail = new SimpleMailMessage();

        mail.setFrom(from);
        mail.setTo(recipient);
        mail.setSubject("UTEFit - Mã OTP " + reason);

        mail.setText(
                "Mã xác thực UTEFit của bạn: " + code
                        + "\n\nMã có hiệu lực trong 5 phút."
                        + "\nKhông chia sẻ mã này cho người khác."
        );

        sender.send(mail);
    }
}

FILE: E:\Nhom22_DeTai20_UTEFit\src\main\java\com\utefit\service\impl\OtpServiceImpl.java
============================================================
package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.entity.OtpCode;
import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.enums.OtpType;
import com.utefit.repository.OtpCodeRepository;
import com.utefit.service.MailService;
import com.utefit.service.OtpService;

import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

@Service
public class OtpServiceImpl implements OtpService {

    private static final SecureRandom RANDOM =
            new SecureRandom();

    private final OtpCodeRepository repository;
    private final PasswordEncoder encoder;
    private final MailService mailService;

    public OtpServiceImpl(
            OtpCodeRepository repository,
            PasswordEncoder encoder,
            MailService mailService
    ) {
        this.repository = repository;
        this.encoder = encoder;
        this.mailService = mailService;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void issueAndSend(User user, OtpType type) {

        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

        // Giới hạn tối đa 5 OTP trong một giờ.
        long sent = repository
                .countByUserAndTypeAndCreatedAtAfter(
                        user,
                        type,
                        now.minusHours(1)
                );

        if (sent >= 5) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Đã gửi quá nhiều OTP. Vui lòng thử lại sau."
            );
        }

        // Mỗi lần gửi OTP phải cách nhau 60 giây.
        repository
                .findTopByUserAndTypeOrderByCreatedAtDesc(
                        user,
                        type
                )
                .ifPresent(last -> {
                    if (last.getCreatedAt()
                            .isAfter(now.minusSeconds(60))) {

                        throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Vui lòng chờ 60 giây trước khi gửi lại OTP."
                        );
                    }
                });

        // Vô hiệu các OTP cũ cùng loại.
        List<OtpCode> oldCodes =
                repository.findByUserAndTypeAndUsedFalse(
                        user,
                        type
                );

        for (OtpCode old : oldCodes) {
            old.setUsed(true);
            old.setUsedAt(now);
        }

        repository.saveAll(oldCodes);

        // Sinh 6 chữ số bằng SecureRandom.
        String code = String.format(
                "%06d",
                RANDOM.nextInt(1_000_000)
        );

        OtpCode otp = OtpCode.builder()
                .user(user)
                .type(type)
                .code(encoder.encode(code))
                .expiredAt(now.plusMinutes(5))
                .used(false)
                .attemptCount(0)
                .createdAt(now)
                .build();

        repository.save(otp);

        String reason = type == OtpType.REGISTER
                ? "đăng ký"
                : "đặt lại mật khẩu";

        mailService.sendOtp(
                user.getEmail(),
                code,
                reason
        );
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public boolean consume(
            User user,
            OtpType type,
            String code
    ) {
        var optional = repository
                .findTopByUserAndTypeAndUsedFalseOrderByCreatedAtDesc(
                        user,
                        type
                );

        if (optional.isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }

        OtpCode otp = optional.get();
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

        // OTP hết hạn.
        if (!otp.getExpiredAt().isAfter(now)) {
            otp.setUsed(true);
            otp.setUsedAt(now);
            return false;
        }

        int attempts = otp.getAttemptCount() == null
                ? 0
                : otp.getAttemptCount();

        // Quá số lần nhập sai.
        if (attempts >= 5) {
            return false;
        }

        // OTP sai.
        if (!encoder.matches(code, otp.getCode())) {

            attempts++;
            otp.setAttemptCount(attempts);

            if (attempts >= 5) {
                otp.setUsed(true);
                otp.setUsedAt(now);
            }

            return false;
        }

        // OTP đúng: đánh dấu đã dùng.
        otp.setUsed(true);
        otp.setUsedAt(now);

        return true;
    }
}

package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.dto.trainerrequest.TrainerRequestResponse;
import com.utefit.entity.Trainer;
import com.utefit.entity.TrainerRequest;
import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.enums.TrainerRequestStatus;
import com.utefit.enums.UserRole;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import com.utefit.repository.TrainerRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.TrainerRequestRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;
import com.utefit.service.TrainerRequestService;
import jakarta.persistence.EntityNotFoundException;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Transactional
public class TrainerRequestServiceImpl implements TrainerRequestService {

    private final TrainerRequestRepository trainerRequestRepository;
    private final TrainerRepository trainerRepository;
    private final UserRepository userRepository;

    @Override
    public TrainerRequestResponse createRequest(Long memberUserId,
                                                Long trainerId,
                                                String message) {

        User member = userRepository.findById(memberUserId)
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new EntityNotFoundException("Không tìm thấy thành viên"));

        if (member.getRole() != UserRole.MEMBER) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Chỉ thành viên mới được gửi yêu cầu PT.");
        }

        Trainer trainer = trainerRepository.findById(trainerId)
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new EntityNotFoundException("Không tìm thấy huấn luyện viên."));

        User trainerUser = trainer.getUser();

        // Lấy trạng thái active/enabled an toàn cho cả Boolean và boolean
        boolean trainerActive = Boolean.TRUE.equals(trainer.getActive());
        boolean trainerUserEnabled = Boolean.TRUE.equals(trainerUser.getEnabled());

        // Chặn PT inactive / user khoá / không phải PT
        if (!trainerActive
                || !trainerUserEnabled
                || trainerUser.getStatus() != UserStatus.ACTIVE
                || trainerUser.getRole() != UserRole.PT) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Huấn luyện viên hiện không khả dụng.");
        }

        // Chặn duplicate PENDING
        boolean existedPending = trainerRequestRepository
                .existsByMember_IdAndTrainer_IdAndStatus(
                        memberUserId,
                        trainerId,
                        TrainerRequestStatus.PENDING
                );

        if (existedPending) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    "Bạn đã có một yêu cầu đang chờ phản hồi với huấn luyện viên này."
            );
        }

        TrainerRequest entity = TrainerRequest.builder()
                .member(member)
                .trainer(trainer)
                .message(message)
                .status(TrainerRequestStatus.PENDING)
                .requestedAt(LocalDateTime.now())
                .build();

        TrainerRequest saved = trainerRequestRepository.save(entity);

        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<TrainerRequestResponse> getRequestsForMember(Long memberUserId,
                                                             Pageable pageable) {
        return trainerRequestRepository
                .findByMember_IdOrderByRequestedAtDesc(memberUserId, pageable)
                .map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<TrainerRequestResponse> getRequestsForTrainer(Long trainerUserId,
                                                              Pageable pageable) {
        return trainerRequestRepository
                .findByTrainer_User_IdOrderByRequestedAtDesc(trainerUserId, pageable)
                .map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    public void acceptRequest(Long trainerUserId, Long requestId) {
        changeStatus(trainerUserId, requestId, TrainerRequestStatus.ACCEPTED);
    }

    @Override
    public void rejectRequest(Long trainerUserId, Long requestId) {
        changeStatus(trainerUserId, requestId, TrainerRequestStatus.REJECTED);
    }

    private void changeStatus(Long trainerUserId,
                              Long requestId,
                              TrainerRequestStatus targetStatus) {

        TrainerRequest request = trainerRequestRepository
                .findByIdAndTrainer_User_Id(requestId, trainerUserId)
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new EntityNotFoundException("Không tìm thấy yêu cầu hoặc bạn không có quyền xử lý."));

        if (request.getStatus() != TrainerRequestStatus.PENDING) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Yêu cầu này đã được xử lý trước đó.");
        }

        request.setStatus(targetStatus);
        request.setRespondedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        // @Transactional sẽ tự flush
    }

    private TrainerRequestResponse toResponse(TrainerRequest entity) {
        User member = entity.getMember();
        Trainer trainer = entity.getTrainer();
        User trainerUser = trainer.getUser();

        String memberName = buildFullName(member.getLastName(), member.getFirstName());
        String trainerName = buildFullName(trainerUser.getLastName(), trainerUser.getFirstName());

        return TrainerRequestResponse.builder()
                .id(entity.getId())

                .memberId(member.getId())
                .memberName(memberName)
                .memberEmail(member.getEmail())

                .trainerId(trainer.getId())
                .trainerName(trainerName)
                .trainerEmail(trainerUser.getEmail())

                .message(entity.getMessage())
                .status(entity.getStatus())
                .requestedAt(entity.getRequestedAt())
                .respondedAt(entity.getRespondedAt())
                .build();
    }

    private String buildFullName(String lastName, String firstName) {
        String ln = lastName != null ? lastName.trim() : "";
        String fn = firstName != null ? firstName.trim() : "";
        return (ln + " " + fn).trim();
    }
}

package com.utefit.service.impl;

import com.utefit.dto.trainer.BranchOptionResponse;
import com.utefit.dto.trainer.TrainerProfileUpdateRequest;
import com.utefit.dto.trainer.TrainerResponse;
import com.utefit.dto.trainer.TrainerSearchRequest;
import com.utefit.entity.Branch;
import com.utefit.entity.Trainer;
import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.enums.UserRole;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import com.utefit.repository.BranchRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.TrainerRepository;
import com.utefit.service.TrainerService;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import java.util.List;

@Service
public class TrainerServiceImpl implements TrainerService {

    private final TrainerRepository trainerRepository;
    private final BranchRepository branchRepository;

    public TrainerServiceImpl(TrainerRepository trainerRepository,
                              BranchRepository branchRepository) {
        this.trainerRepository = trainerRepository;
        this.branchRepository = branchRepository;
    }

   
    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<TrainerResponse> searchPublicTrainers(TrainerSearchRequest request) {

        if (request == null) {
            request = new TrainerSearchRequest();
        }

        String keyword = normalize(request.getKeyword());

        int page = request.getPage() == null
                ? 0
                : Math.max(0, request.getPage());

        int size = 6;

        PageRequest pageable = PageRequest.of(
                page,
                size,
                Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "id")  
        );

        return trainerRepository
                .searchPublicTrainers(
                        keyword,
                        request.getBranchId(),
                        UserStatus.ACTIVE,
                        UserRole.PT,
                        pageable
                )
                .map(this::toResponse);
    }

   
    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public TrainerResponse getPublicTrainerById(Long id) {

        Trainer trainer = trainerRepository
                .findPublicTrainerById(
                        id,
                        UserStatus.ACTIVE,
                        UserRole.PT
                )
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResponseStatusException(
                        HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                        "Không tìm thấy huấn luyện viên"
                ));

        return toResponse(trainer);
    }

    
    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<BranchOptionResponse> getActiveBranchOptions() {

        return branchRepository
                .findByActiveTrueOrderByNameAsc()
                .stream()
                .map(branch -> new BranchOptionResponse(
                        branch.getId(),
                        branch.getName()
                ))
                .toList();
    }

    
    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public TrainerResponse getMyProfile(Long userId) {

        Trainer trainer = trainerRepository
                .findByUserId(userId, UserRole.PT)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResponseStatusException(
                        HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                        "Không tìm thấy hồ sơ PT"
                ));

        return toResponse(trainer);
    }

   
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public TrainerResponse updateMyProfile(Long userId,
                                           TrainerProfileUpdateRequest request) {

        Trainer trainer = trainerRepository
                .findByUserId(userId, UserRole.PT)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResponseStatusException(
                        HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                        "Không tìm thấy hồ sơ PT"
                ));

        trainer.setSpecialization(normalize(request.getSpecialization()));
        trainer.setYearsExperience(request.getYearsExperience());
        trainer.setBio(normalize(request.getBio()));

        trainerRepository.save(trainer);

        return toResponse(trainer);
    }

    
    private TrainerResponse toResponse(Trainer trainer) {

        User user = trainer.getUser();
        Branch branch = trainer.getBranch();

        return TrainerResponse.builder()
                .id(trainer.getId())
                .fullName(user.getFirstName() + " " + user.getLastName())
                .avatarUrl(user.getAvatarUrl())
                .specialization(trainer.getSpecialization())
                .yearsExperience(trainer.getYearsExperience())
                .bio(trainer.getBio())
                .branchId(branch != null ? branch.getId() : null)
                .branchName(branch != null ? branch.getName() : null)
                .branchAddress(branch != null ? branch.getAddress() : null)
                .build();
    }

    
    private String normalize(String value) {

        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }

        return value.trim();
    }
}

package com.utefit.service;

import com.utefit.dto.cloudinary.MediaUploadResult;
import com.utefit.dto.member.PasswordChangeRequest;
import com.utefit.dto.member.ProfileUpdateRequest;
import com.utefit.entity.User;
import com.utefit.enums.UserRole;
import com.utefit.enums.UserStatus;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;

import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

@Service
public class MemberProfileService {

    private final UserRepository users;
    private final PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;
    private final CloudinaryService cloudinaryService;

    public MemberProfileService(
            UserRepository users,
            PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder,
            CloudinaryService cloudinaryService
    ) {
        this.users = users;
        this.passwordEncoder = passwordEncoder;
        this.cloudinaryService = cloudinaryService;
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public ProfileView getProfile(String email) {
        User user = currentMember(email);

        return new ProfileView(
                user.getId(),
                user.getEmail(),
                user.getFirstName(),
                user.getLastName(),
                user.getPhone(),
                user.getAvatarUrl()
        );
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public ProfileUpdateRequest getEditForm(String email) {
        User user = currentMember(email);

        ProfileUpdateRequest form =
                new ProfileUpdateRequest();

        form.setFirstName(user.getFirstName());
        form.setLastName(user.getLastName());
        form.setPhone(user.getPhone());

        return form;
    }

    @Transactional
    public void updateProfile(
            String email,
            ProfileUpdateRequest request
    ) {
        User user = currentMember(email);

        user.setFirstName(request.getFirstName().trim());
        user.setLastName(request.getLastName().trim());

        String phone = request.getPhone();
        user.setPhone(
                phone == null || phone.isBlank()
                        ? null
                        : phone.trim()
        );

        users.save(user);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void changePassword(
            String email,
            PasswordChangeRequest request
    ) {
        User user = currentMember(email);

        if (!passwordEncoder.matches(
                request.getCurrentPassword(),
                user.getPassword()
        )) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Mật khẩu hiện tại không đúng"
            );
        }

        if (!request.getNewPassword().equals(
                request.getConfirmPassword()
        )) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Xác nhận mật khẩu mới không khớp"
            );
        }

        if (passwordEncoder.matches(
                request.getNewPassword(),
                user.getPassword()
        )) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Mật khẩu mới phải khác mật khẩu cũ"
            );
        }

        user.setPassword(
                passwordEncoder.encode(
                        request.getNewPassword()
                )
        );

        // Vô hiệu hóa các JWT cũ.
        int version = user.getTokenVersion() == null
                ? 0
                : user.getTokenVersion();

        user.setTokenVersion(version + 1);
        users.save(user);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void updateAvatar(
            String email,
            MultipartFile file
    ) {
        User user = currentMember(email);

        if (file == null || file.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Vui lòng chọn ảnh đại diện"
            );
        }

        // Dùng CloudinaryService của B, không viết
        // một service upload thứ hai.
        MediaUploadResult uploaded =
                cloudinaryService.uploadImage(
                        file,
                        "utefit/member-avatars"
                );

        user.setAvatarUrl(uploaded.url());
        user.setAvatarPublicId(uploaded.publicId());

        users.save(user);
    }

    private User currentMember(String email) {
        User user = users.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Không tìm thấy tài khoản"
                        )
                );

        if (user.getRole() != UserRole.MEMBER
                || user.getStatus() != UserStatus.ACTIVE
                || !Boolean.TRUE.equals(user.getEnabled())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Tài khoản không có quyền thao tác"
            );
        }

        return user;
    }

    public record ProfileView(
            Long id,
            String email,
            String firstName,
            String lastName,
            String phone,
            String avatarUrl
    ) {}
}

package com.utefit.service;

import com.utefit.dto.member.MembershipPurchaseRequest;
import com.utefit.entity.*;
import com.utefit.enums.*;

import com.utefit.repository.BranchRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.MembershipPlanRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.MembershipRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.PaymentRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.UserRepository;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

@Service
public class MembershipPaymentService {

    private final UserRepository users;
    private final BranchRepository branches;
    private final MembershipPlanRepository plans;
    private final MembershipRepository memberships;
    private final PaymentRepository payments;

    public MembershipPaymentService(
            UserRepository users,
            BranchRepository branches,
            MembershipPlanRepository plans,
            MembershipRepository memberships,
            PaymentRepository payments
    ) {
        this.users = users;
        this.branches = branches;
        this.plans = plans;
        this.memberships = memberships;
        this.payments = payments;
    }

    // Member chọn chi nhánh + gói.
    // Chưa thanh toán => PENDING.
    @Transactional
    public Long createMembership(
            String email,
            MembershipPurchaseRequest request
    ) {
        User user = currentMember(email);

        Branch branch = branches.findById(
                request.getBranchId()
        ).orElseThrow(() ->
                new IllegalArgumentException(
                        "Chi nhánh không tồn tại"
                )
        );

        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(branch.getActive())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Chi nhánh đang ngừng hoạt động"
            );
        }

        MembershipPlan plan = plans.findById(
                request.getPlanId()
        ).orElseThrow(() ->
                new IllegalArgumentException(
                        "Gói tập không tồn tại"
                )
        );

        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(plan.getActive())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Gói tập đang ngừng kinh doanh"
            );
        }

        if (plan.getDurationDays() == null
                || plan.getDurationDays() <= 0
                || plan.getPrice() == null
                || plan.getPrice().compareTo(
                BigDecimal.ZERO
        ) <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Thông tin gói tập không hợp lệ"
            );
        }

        if (memberships.existsByUser_IdAndStatus(
                user.getId(),
                MembershipStatus.PENDING
        )) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Bạn đang có gói tập chờ thanh toán"
            );
        }

        if (memberships
                .existsByUser_IdAndStatusAndEndDateGreaterThanEqual(
                        user.getId(),
                        MembershipStatus.ACTIVE,
                        LocalDate.now()
                )) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Bạn đang có gói tập còn hiệu lực"
            );
        }

        Membership membership = Membership.builder()
                .user(user)
                .branch(branch)
                .plan(plan)
                .status(MembershipStatus.PENDING)
                .build();

        return memberships.save(membership).getId();
    }

    // Member tạo yêu cầu thanh toán CASH.
    // Chưa thu tiền => Payment vẫn PENDING.
    @Transactional
    public Long requestCashPayment(
            String email,
            Long membershipId
    ) {
        User member = currentMember(email);

        Membership membership = memberships
                .lockById(membershipId)
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Không tìm thấy gói đăng ký"
                        )
                );

        if (!membership.getUser().getId()
                .equals(member.getId())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Bạn không sở hữu gói đăng ký này"
            );
        }

        if (membership.getStatus()
                != MembershipStatus.PENDING) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Gói này không chờ thanh toán"
            );
        }

        if (payments.existsByMembership_IdAndStatus(
                membershipId,
                PaymentStatus.PENDING
        )) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Yêu cầu thanh toán đã được tạo"
            );
        }

        MembershipPlan plan = membership.getPlan();

        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(plan.getActive())
                || !Boolean.TRUE.equals(
                membership.getBranch().getActive()
        )) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Chi nhánh hoặc gói tập đã ngừng hoạt động"
            );
        }

        Payment payment = Payment.builder()
                .user(member)
                .membership(membership)
                .amount(plan.getPrice())
                .method(PaymentMethod.CASH)
                .status(PaymentStatus.PENDING)
                .build();

        return payments.save(payment).getId();
    }

    // CHỈ ADMIN được gọi thông qua /admin/**.
    // Admin xác nhận SAU KHI đã thu tiền tại quầy.
    @Transactional
    public void confirmCashPayment(Long paymentId) {

        Payment payment = payments.lockById(paymentId)
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Không tìm thấy giao dịch"
                        )
                );

        if (payment.getMethod() != PaymentMethod.CASH) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Chỉ xác nhận thanh toán CASH"
            );
        }

        if (payment.getStatus() != PaymentStatus.PENDING) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Giao dịch không ở trạng thái PENDING"
            );
        }

        if (payment.getMembership() == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Giao dịch không có thông tin gói tập"
            );
        }

        Membership membership = memberships
                .lockById(payment.getMembership().getId())
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Gói tập không tồn tại"
                        )
                );

        if (membership.getStatus()
                != MembershipStatus.PENDING) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Gói tập không ở trạng thái PENDING"
            );
        }

        if (!payment.getUser().getId()
                .equals(membership.getUser().getId())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Chủ giao dịch không khớp chủ gói tập"
            );
        }

        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(
                membership.getBranch().getActive()
        ) || !Boolean.TRUE.equals(
                membership.getPlan().getActive()
        )) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Chi nhánh hoặc gói tập không còn hoạt động"
            );
        }

        int duration = membership.getPlan()
                .getDurationDays();

        if (duration <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Thời hạn gói tập không hợp lệ"
            );
        }

        if (payment.getAmount() == null
                || payment.getAmount().compareTo(
                BigDecimal.ZERO
        ) <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Số tiền giao dịch không hợp lệ"
            );
        }

        LocalDate start = LocalDate.now();

        membership.setStartDate(start);
        membership.setEndDate(start.plusDays(duration));
        membership.setStatus(MembershipStatus.ACTIVE);

        payment.setStatus(PaymentStatus.PAID);
        payment.setPaidAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        payment.setTransactionCode(
                "CASH-" + payment.getId()
        );

        memberships.save(membership);
        payments.save(payment);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<MembershipView> myMemberships(
            String email
    ) {
        User member = currentMember(email);

        return memberships
                .findByUser_IdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(
                        member.getId()
                )
                .stream()
                .map(m -> new MembershipView(
                        m.getId(),
                        m.getBranch().getName(),
                        m.getPlan().getName(),
                        m.getPlan().getPrice(),
                        m.getStatus().name(),
                        m.getStartDate(),
                        m.getEndDate()
                ))
                .toList();
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<PaymentView> myPayments(String email) {
        User member = currentMember(email);

        return payments
                .findByUser_IdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(
                        member.getId()
                )
                .stream()
                .map(p -> new PaymentView(
                        p.getId(),
                        p.getMembership() == null
                                ? "Không có"
                                : p.getMembership()
                                .getPlan().getName(),
                        p.getAmount(),
                        p.getMethod().name(),
                        p.getStatus().name(),
                        p.getTransactionCode(),
                        p.getCreatedAt(),
                        p.getPaidAt()
                ))
                .toList();
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<PendingCashView> pendingCashPayments() {

        return payments
                .findByStatusAndMethodOrderByCreatedAtAsc(
                        PaymentStatus.PENDING,
                        PaymentMethod.CASH
                )
                .stream()
                .map(p -> new PendingCashView(
                        p.getId(),
                        p.getUser().getEmail(),
                        p.getMembership().getPlan().getName(),
                        p.getMembership().getBranch().getName(),
                        p.getAmount(),
                        p.getCreatedAt()
                ))
                .toList();
    }

    private User currentMember(String email) {
        User user = users.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Không tìm thấy tài khoản"
                        )
                );

        if (user.getRole() != UserRole.MEMBER
                || user.getStatus() != UserStatus.ACTIVE
                || !Boolean.TRUE.equals(user.getEnabled())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Tài khoản không hợp lệ"
            );
        }

        return user;
    }

    public record MembershipView(
            Long id,
            String branchName,
            String planName,
            BigDecimal price,
            String status,
            LocalDate startDate,
            LocalDate endDate
    ) {}

    public record PaymentView(
            Long id,
            String planName,
            BigDecimal amount,
            String method,
            String status,
            String transactionCode,
            LocalDateTime createdAt,
            LocalDateTime paidAt
    ) {}

    public record PendingCashView(
            Long id,
            String memberEmail,
            String planName,
            String branchName,
            BigDecimal amount,
            LocalDateTime createdAt
    ) {}
}

package com.utefit.service;

import com.utefit.entity.Branch;
import com.utefit.entity.FitnessClass;
import com.utefit.entity.MembershipPlan;
import com.utefit.enums.ClassStatus;
import com.utefit.repository.BranchRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.MembershipPlanRepository;
import com.utefit.repository.PublicFitnessClassRepository;

import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Set;

@Service
public class PublicCatalogService {

    private static final int PAGE_SIZE = 6;
    private static final Set<ClassStatus> PUBLIC_STATUSES =
            Set.of(ClassStatus.OPEN, ClassStatus.FULL);

    private final BranchRepository branches;
    private final MembershipPlanRepository plans;
    private final PublicFitnessClassRepository classes;

    public PublicCatalogService(
            BranchRepository branches,
            MembershipPlanRepository plans,
            PublicFitnessClassRepository classes
    ) {
        this.branches = branches;
        this.plans = plans;
        this.classes = classes;
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<BranchView> searchBranches(
            String keyword, int page
    ) {
        return branches.searchBranches(
                normalize(keyword),
                true,
                pageable(page)
        ).map(this::toBranchView);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public BranchView branchDetail(Long id) {
        Branch branch = branches.findById(id)
                .filter(b -> Boolean.TRUE.equals(b.getActive()))
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResponseStatusException(
                        HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                        "Không tìm thấy chi nhánh"
                ));

        return toBranchView(branch);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<PlanView> searchPlans(
            String keyword, int page
    ) {
        return plans.searchPlans(
                normalize(keyword),
                true,
                pageable(page)
        ).map(this::toPlanView);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<ClassView> searchClasses(
            String keyword, Long branchId, int page
    ) {
        return classes.searchPublicClasses(
                normalize(keyword),
                branchId,
                PUBLIC_STATUSES,
                pageable(page)
        ).map(this::toClassView);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<BranchView> featuredBranches() {
        return searchBranches(null, 0)
                .getContent()
                .stream()
                .limit(3)
                .toList();
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<PlanView> featuredPlans() {
        return searchPlans(null, 0)
                .getContent()
                .stream()
                .limit(3)
                .toList();
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<ClassView> featuredClasses() {
        return searchClasses(null, null, 0)
                .getContent()
                .stream()
                .limit(3)
                .toList();
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<BranchView> activeBranchOptions() {
        return branches.findByActiveTrueOrderByNameAsc()
                .stream()
                .map(this::toBranchView)
                .toList();
    }

    private PageRequest pageable(int page) {
        return PageRequest.of(
                Math.max(0, Math.min(page, 10000)),
                PAGE_SIZE,
                Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "id")
        );
    }

    private String normalize(String keyword) {
        if (keyword == null || keyword.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }

        String result = keyword.trim()
                .toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);

        if (result.length() > 100) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Từ khóa tối đa 100 ký tự"
            );
        }

        return "%" + result + "%";
    }

    private BranchView toBranchView(Branch b) {
        return new BranchView(
                b.getId(),
                b.getName(),
                b.getAddress(),
                b.getPhone(),
                b.getDescription(),
                b.getImageUrl(),
                b.getOpeningTime(),
                b.getClosingTime()
        );
    }

    private PlanView toPlanView(MembershipPlan p) {
        return new PlanView(
                p.getId(),
                p.getName(),
                p.getDescription(),
                p.getDurationDays(),
                p.getPrice()
        );
    }

    private ClassView toClassView(FitnessClass c) {
        String trainerName = "Đang cập nhật";

        if (c.getTrainer() != null
                && c.getTrainer().getUser() != null) {
            var user = c.getTrainer().getUser();
            trainerName = user.getLastName()
                    + " " + user.getFirstName();
        }

        return new ClassView(
                c.getId(),
                c.getName(),
                c.getDescription(),
                c.getBranch().getName(),
                c.getCategory().getName(),
                trainerName,
                c.getCapacity(),
                c.getStatus().name()
        );
    }

    public record BranchView(
            Long id,
            String name,
            String address,
            String phone,
            String description,
            String imageUrl,
            LocalTime openingTime,
            LocalTime closingTime
    ) {}

    public record PlanView(
            Long id,
            String name,
            String description,
            Integer durationDays,
            BigDecimal price
    ) {}

    public record ClassView(
            Long id,
            String name,
            String description,
            String branchName,
            String categoryName,
            String trainerName,
            Integer capacity,
            String status
    ) {}
}

package com.utefit.validation;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

import javax.imageio.ImageIO;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Set;

@Component
public class ImageValidator {

    private static final long MAX_FILE_SIZE =
            5L * 1024 * 1024;

    private static final Set<String> ALLOWED_TYPES =
            Set.of(
                    "image/jpeg",
                    "image/png",
                    "image/gif"
            );

    private static final Set<String> ALLOWED_FORMATS =
            Set.of(
                    "JPEG",
                    "PNG",
                    "GIF"
            );

    public void validate(MultipartFile file) {

        if (file == null || file.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Vui lòng chọn ảnh để tải lên"
            );
        }

        if (file.getSize() > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Dung lượng ảnh không được vượt quá 5MB"
            );
        }

        String contentType = file.getContentType();

        if (contentType == null ||
                !ALLOWED_TYPES.contains(
                        contentType.toLowerCase()
                )) {

            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Chỉ hỗ trợ ảnh JPG, PNG và GIF"
            );
        }

        try {

            byte[] data = file.getBytes();

            try (var input = ImageIO.createImageInputStream(
                    new ByteArrayInputStream(data)
            )) {

                if (input == null) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                            "Không thể đọc dữ liệu ảnh"
                    );
                }

                var readers = ImageIO.getImageReaders(input);

                if (!readers.hasNext()) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                            "File không phải ảnh hợp lệ"
                    );
                }

                var reader = readers.next();

                try {

                    String format =
                            reader.getFormatName().toUpperCase();

                    if (!ALLOWED_FORMATS.contains(format)) {
                        throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Định dạng ảnh không được hỗ trợ"
                        );
                    }

                    reader.setInput(input);

                    int width = reader.getWidth(0);
                    int height = reader.getHeight(0);

                    if (width <= 0 || height <= 0 ||
                            width > 10000 || height > 10000) {

                        throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Kích thước ảnh không hợp lệ"
                        );
                    }

                    boolean matches = switch (format) {
                        case "JPEG" -> contentType.equalsIgnoreCase("image/jpeg");
                        case "PNG" -> contentType.equalsIgnoreCase("image/png");
                        case "GIF" -> contentType.equalsIgnoreCase("image/gif");
                        default -> false;
                    };

                    if (!matches) {
                        throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                                "Định dạng ảnh không khớp với loại file"
                        );
                    }

                } finally {
                    reader.dispose();
                }
            }

        } catch (IOException e) {

            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Không thể kiểm tra file ảnh",
                    e
            );
        }
    }
}

/**
 * UTEFit - Trainers Page
 *
 * Features:
 * - Scroll reveal animation
 * - Smooth scrolling
 * - Respect reduced-motion preference
 */

(function () {
    "use strict";

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

        initScrollReveal();

        initSmoothScroll();

    });


    /**
     * Scroll Reveal Animation
     */
    function initScrollReveal() {

        const elements = document.querySelectorAll(
            ".reveal-on-scroll"
        );

        if (elements.length === 0) {
            return;
        }

        const prefersReducedMotion = window.matchMedia(
            "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)"
        ).matches;

        if (prefersReducedMotion) {
            return;
        }

        if (!("IntersectionObserver" in window)) {
            return;
        }

        const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
            function (entries, currentObserver) {

                entries.forEach(function (entry) {

                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {

                        entry.target.classList.add("visible");

                        currentObserver.unobserve(entry.target);

                    }

                });

            },
            {
                threshold: 0.08,
                rootMargin: "0px 0px 40px 0px"
            }
        );

        elements.forEach(function (element) {

            // Only hide elements when JavaScript is available.
            element.classList.add("will-animate");

            observer.observe(element);

        });

    }


    /**
     * Smooth Scroll
     */
    function initSmoothScroll() {

        const links = document.querySelectorAll(
            'a[href^="#"]'
        );

        links.forEach(function (link) {

            link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {

                const targetId = link.getAttribute("href");

                if (!targetId || targetId === "#") {
                    return;
                }

                const targetElement = document.querySelector(
                    targetId
                );

                if (!targetElement) {
                    return;
                }

                event.preventDefault();

                const prefersReducedMotion = window.matchMedia(
                    "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)"
                ).matches;

                targetElement.scrollIntoView({
                    behavior: prefersReducedMotion
                        ? "auto"
                        : "smooth",

                    block: "start"
                });

            });

        });

    }

})();

<!-- Script lọc động danh sách PT theo Chi nhánh và Loại lớp -->
<script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
        const branchSelect = document.getElementById("branchId");
        const categorySelect = document.getElementById("categoryId");
        const trainerSelect = document.getElementById("trainerSelect");

        if (!branchSelect || !categorySelect || !trainerSelect) return;

        function filterTrainers() {
            const selectedBranchId = branchSelect.value;
            const selectedCategoryText = categorySelect.options[categorySelect.selectedIndex]?.text.trim().toLowerCase() || "";
            const isCategorySelected = categorySelect.value !== "";

            const options = trainerSelect.querySelectorAll("option");
            let currentSelectedStillValid = false;

            options.forEach(opt => {
                if (opt.value === "") {
                    opt.hidden = false;
                    opt.disabled = false;
                    return;
                }

                const ptBranchId = opt.getAttribute("data-branch-id") || "";
                const ptSpec = (opt.getAttribute("data-spec") || "").toLowerCase();

                // Điều kiện lọc: Khớp chi nhánh (hoặc PT tự do) và phù hợp chuyên môn
                const matchBranch = !selectedBranchId || (ptBranchId === selectedBranchId || ptBranchId === "");
                const matchCategory = !isCategorySelected ||
                    ptSpec.includes(selectedCategoryText) ||
                    selectedCategoryText.includes(ptSpec) ||
                    ptSpec.includes("general") ||
                    ptSpec.includes("đa năng");

                if (matchBranch && matchCategory) {
                    opt.hidden = false;
                    opt.disabled = false;
                    if (opt.value === trainerSelect.value) {
                        currentSelectedStillValid = true;
                    }
                } else {
                    opt.hidden = true;
                    opt.disabled = true;
                }
            });

            if (!currentSelectedStillValid && trainerSelect.value !== "") {
                trainerSelect.value = "";
            }
        }

        branchSelect.addEventListener("change", filterTrainers);
        categorySelect.addEventListener("change", filterTrainers);

        // Chạy lần đầu khi load trang
        filterTrainers();
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

        }

        /* ==================== SIDEBAR CAO CẤP ==================== */

            min-height: 100vh;
            width: 280px;
            background: linear-gradient(180deg, #0b1120 0%, #0f172a 100%);
            color: #94a3b8;
            border-right: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.07);
            box-shadow: 4px 0 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);
            z-index: 100;
        }

        .utefit-admin-logo {
            font-size: 1.45rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            letter-spacing: 0.5px;
            color: #ffffff;
            text-decoration: none;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 10px;
        }

        .utefit-admin-logo .brand-symbol {
            color: var(--utefit-accent);
            font-size: 1.4rem;

                        </li>

                        </li>
                        <li class="page-item" th:classappend="${planPage.last} ? 'disabled'">
                            <a class="page-link" th:href="@{/admin/membership-plans(page=${planPage.number + 1}, keyword=${keyword}, active=${selectedActive})}">Sau</a>
                        </li>
