                                    <span th:text="${t.yearsExperience != null ? t.yearsExperience + ' năm kinh nghiệm' : 'Chưa cập nhật'}">5 năm</span>
                                </div>
                            </td>
                            <td>
                                <!-- Form gán/đổi Chi nhánh nhanh ngay trên từng dòng -->
                                <form th:action="@{/admin/trainers/{id}/assign-branch(id=${t.id})}" method="post" class="d-flex gap-2 align-items-center m-0">
                                    <select name="branchId" class="form-select form-select-sm rounded-3">
                                        <option value="">-- Chưa gán chi nhánh --</option>
                                        <option th:each="b : ${branches}"
                                                th:value="${b.id}"
                                                th:text="${b.name + (!b.active ? ' (Ngưng)' : '')}"
                                                th:selected="${t.branch != null and t.branch.id == b.id}">
                                        </option>
                                    </select>
                                    <button type="submit" class="btn btn-sm btn-utefit rounded-3 px-2" title="Lưu chi nhánh">
                                        <i class="bi bi-check2-circle"></i> Lưu
                                    </button>
                                </form>
                            </td>
                            <td>
                                <span th:if="${t.active}" class="status-pill bg-success bg-opacity-10 text-success">
                                    <span class="dot bg-success"></span> Hoạt động
                                </span>
                                <span th:unless="${t.active}" class="status-pill bg-secondary bg-opacity-10 text-secondary">
                                    <span class="dot bg-secondary"></span> Tạm ngưng
                                </span>
                            </td>
                            <td class="text-end pe-4">
                                <a th:href="@{/trainers/{id}(id=${t.id})}" target="_blank"
                                   class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-dark rounded-pill px-3 me-1" title="Xem trang public">
                                    <i class="bi bi-box-arrow-up-right"></i>
                                </a>
                                <form th:action="@{/admin/trainers/{id}/toggle-active(id=${t.id})}" method="post" class="d-inline">
                                    <button type="submit" class="btn btn-sm rounded-pill px-3"
                                            th:classappend="${t.active ? 'btn-outline-warning' : 'btn-outline-success'}">
                                        <span th:text="${t.active ? 'Tắt PT' : 'Bật PT'}"></span>
                                    </button>
                                </form>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <div class="card-footer bg-white d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center px-4 py-3" th:if="${trainerPage.totalPages > 1}">
                    <span class="small text-muted">Trang <b th:text="${trainerPage.number + 1}"></b> / <b th:text="${trainerPage.totalPages}"></b></span>
                    <ul class="pagination pagination-sm mb-0">
                        <li class="page-item" th:classappend="${trainerPage.first} ? 'disabled'">
                            <a class="page-link" th:href="@{/admin/trainers(page=${trainerPage.number - 1}, keyword=${keyword}, branchId=${selectedBranchId}, active=${selectedActive})}">Trước</a>
                        </li>
                        <li class="page-item" th:each="i : ${#numbers.sequence(0, trainerPage.totalPages - 1)}"
                            th:classappend="${i == trainerPage.number} ? 'active'">
                            <a class="page-link" th:href="@{/admin/trainers(page=${i}, keyword=${keyword}, branchId=${selectedBranchId}, active=${selectedActive})}" th:text="${i + 1}"></a>
                        </li>
                        <li class="page-item" th:classappend="${trainerPage.last} ? 'disabled'">

        đặt lại mật khẩu.
    </p>

          th:object="${form}"
          method="post">

        <div class="alert alert-danger"
             th:if="${#fields.hasGlobalErrors()}">

            <div th:each="err : ${#fields.globalErrors()}"
                 th:text="${err}">
            </div>

        <div class="mb-3">
            <label class="form-label">
                Email tài khoản
            </label>

            <input type="email"
                   class="form-control"
                   th:field="*{email}"
                   required>

            <div class="text-danger"
                 th:if="${#fields.hasErrors('email')}"
                 th:errors="*{email}">
            </div>
        </div>

        <button type="submit"
                class="btn btn-primary w-100">

            Gửi OTP đặt lại mật khẩu

        </button>

          th:action="@{/branches}"

                    type="submit">

            </button>
        </div>

            <article class="ut-card">

                     class="ut-card-image"
                     th:src="${b.imageUrl}"
                     alt="Ảnh chi nhánh">

                <div th:unless="${b.imageUrl != null and !#strings.isEmpty(b.imageUrl)}"
                     class="ut-empty-image">

                </div>

                        Địa chỉ
                    </p>

                    <p th:if="${b.phone != null}">
                        Điện thoại:
                        <span th:text="${b.phone}"></span>
                    </p>

                    <a class="btn btn-brand mt-2"
                       th:href="@{/branches/{id}(id=${b.id})}">
                        Xem chi tiết
                    </a>

                </div>
            </article>

        </div>

    </div>

    <div th:if="${branchPage.empty}"
         class="ut-panel text-center mt-4">
        Không tìm thấy chi nhánh phù hợp.
    </div>

    <nav th:if="${branchPage.totalPages > 1}"
         class="mt-5"
         aria-label="Phân trang chi nhánh">

        <ul class="pagination ut-pagination justify-content-center flex-wrap">

            <li class="page-item"
                th:classappend="${branchPage.first} ? 'disabled'">

                <a class="page-link"
                   th:href="@{/branches(page=${branchPage.number - 1},keyword=${keyword})}">
                    Trước
                </a>
            </li>

            <li class="page-item"
                th:each="i : ${#numbers.sequence(0,branchPage.totalPages - 1)}"
                th:classappend="${i == branchPage.number} ? 'active'">

                   th:href="@{/branches(page=${i},keyword=${keyword})}"
                   th:text="${i + 1}">
                    1
                </a>
            </li>

            <li class="page-item"
                th:classappend="${branchPage.last} ? 'disabled'">

                <a class="page-link"
                   th:href="@{/branches(page=${branchPage.number + 1},keyword=${keyword})}">
                    Sau
                </a>
            </li>

        </ul>

             th:if="${trainerPage.totalPages > 1}">

            <!-- PREVIOUS -->

                    page=${trainerPage.number - 1},

                aria-label="Trang trước">

                ←

            </a>

                    branchId=${search.branchId}
                )}"
                th:text="${pageNumber + 1}"
                th:classappend="${pageNumber == trainerPage.number} ? ' active' : ''"
                class="pagination-number"
                th:aria-current="${pageNumber == trainerPage.number} ? 'page' : null">

                1

            </a>


            <!-- NEXT -->

                th:if="${trainerPage.hasNext()}"

                    branchId=${search.branchId}
                )}"
                class="pagination-arrow"
                aria-label="Trang tiếp theo">

                →

            </a>

        </nav>

    </div>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="vi"
      xmlns:th="http://www.thymeleaf.org"
      xmlns:sec="http://www.thymeleaf.org/extras/spring-security">

<head th:replace="~{fragments/head :: head('Yêu cầu huấn luyện viên của tôi - UTEFit')}">
</head>

<body>

<header th:replace="~{fragments/header :: header}">
</header>

<main class="py-5 bg-light">

    <div class="container">

        <h1 class="h3 mb-4">
            Yêu cầu huấn luyện viên của tôi
        </h1>

        <div th:if="${successMessage}" class="alert alert-success">
            <span th:text="${successMessage}"></span>
        </div>

        <div th:if="${errorMessage}" class="alert alert-danger">
            <span th:text="${errorMessage}"></span>
        </div>

        <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
            <div class="card-body">

                <div th:if="${requestPage == null or requestPage.empty}">
                    <p class="mb-0 text-muted">
                        Bạn chưa gửi yêu cầu huấn luyện viên nào.
                    </p>
                </div>

                <div th:if="${requestPage != null and !requestPage.empty}">
                    <div class="table-responsive">
                        <table class="table align-middle">
                            <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th>Huấn luyện viên</th>
                                <th>Nội dung</th>
                                <th>Trạng thái</th>
                                <th>Gửi lúc</th>
                                <th>Phản hồi lúc</th>
                            </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                            <tr th:each="req : ${requestPage.content}">
                                <td>
                                    <div class="fw-semibold"
                                         th:text="${req.trainerName}">
                                        Tên PT
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="small text-muted"
                                         th:text="${req.trainerEmail}">
                                        email
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                                <td th:text="${req.message != null ? req.message : '—'}">
                                    Nội dung
                                </td>
                                <td>
                                    <span class="badge rounded-pill"
                                          th:classappend="${req.status.name() == 'PENDING'} ? ' bg-warning text-dark' : 
                                                          (${req.status.name() == 'ACCEPTED'} ? ' bg-success' : ' bg-secondary')"
                                          th:text="${req.status}">
                                        PENDING
                                    </span>
                                </td>
                                <td th:text="${#temporals.format(req.requestedAt, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm')}">
                                    01/01/2026 10:00
                                </td>
                                <td th:text="${req.respondedAt != null ? #temporals.format(req.respondedAt, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : '—'}">
                                    —
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Pagination -->
                    <nav th:if="${requestPage.totalPages > 1}"
                         aria-label="Phân trang yêu cầu PT"
                         class="mt-3">
                        <ul class="pagination mb-0">

                            <li class="page-item"
                                th:classappend="${requestPage.first} ? ' disabled'">
                                <a class="page-link"
                                   th:href="@{/member/trainer-requests(page=${requestPage.number - 1})}">
                                    Trước
                                </a>
                            </li>

                            <li class="page-item"
                                th:each="i : ${#numbers.sequence(0, requestPage.totalPages - 1)}"
                                th:classappend="${i == requestPage.number} ? ' active'">
                                <a class="page-link"
                                   th:text="${i + 1}"
                                   th:href="@{/member/trainer-requests(page=${i})}">
                                    1
                                </a>
                            </li>

                            <li class="page-item"
                                th:classappend="${requestPage.last} ? ' disabled'">
                                <a class="page-link"
                                   th:href="@{/member/trainer-requests(page=${requestPage.number + 1})}">
                                    Sau
                                </a>
                            </li>

                        </ul>
                    </nav>

                </div>

            </div>
        </div>

    </div>

</main>

</footer>

        </h1>

            <span th:text="${errorMessage}"></span>
        </div>

            <div class="card-body">

                    </p>
                </div>

                                <th>Phản hồi lúc</th>

                                    <div class="fw-semibold"
                                         th:text="${req.memberName}">
                                        Tên học viên
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="small text-muted"
                                         th:text="${req.memberEmail}">
                                        email
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                                <td th:text="${req.message != null ? req.message : '—'}">
                                    Nội dung
                                </td>
                                <td>
                                    <span class="badge rounded-pill"
                                          th:classappend="${req.status.name() == 'PENDING'} ? ' bg-warning text-dark' :
                                                          (${req.status.name() == 'ACCEPTED'} ? ' bg-success' : ' bg-secondary')"
                                          th:text="${req.status}">
                                        PENDING
                                    </span>
                                </td>
                                <td th:text="${#temporals.format(req.requestedAt, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm')}">
                                    01/01/2026 10:00
                                </td>
                                <td th:text="${req.respondedAt != null ? #temporals.format(req.respondedAt, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : '—'}">
                                    —
                                </td>
                                <td>
                                    <div th:if="${req.status.name() == 'PENDING'}" class="d-flex gap-2">

                                        <form th:action="@{'/pt/trainer-requests/' + ${req.id} + '/accept'}"
                                              method="post" class="d-inline">
                                            <input type="hidden"
                                                   th:name="${_csrf.parameterName}"
                                                   th:value="${_csrf.token}"/>
                                            <button type="submit"
                                                    class="btn btn-success btn-sm">
                                                Chấp nhận
                                            </button>
                                        </form>

                                        <form th:action="@{'/pt/trainer-requests/' + ${req.id} + '/reject'}"
                                              method="post" class="d-inline">
                                            <input type="hidden"
                                                   th:name="${_csrf.parameterName}"
                                                   th:value="${_csrf.token}"/>
                                            <button type="submit"
                                                    class="btn btn-outline-danger btn-sm">
                                                Từ chối
                                            </button>
                                        </form>

                                    </div>

                                    <div th:if="${req.status.name() != 'PENDING'}"
                                         class="text-muted small">
                                        Đã xử lý
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Pagination -->
                    <nav th:if="${requestPage.totalPages > 1}"
                         aria-label="Phân trang yêu cầu"
                         class="mt-3">
                        <ul class="pagination mb-0">

                            <li class="page-item"
                                th:classappend="${requestPage.first} ? ' disabled'">
                                <a class="page-link"
                                   th:href="@{/pt/trainer-requests(page=${requestPage.number - 1})}">
                                    Trước
                                </a>
                            </li>

                            <li class="page-item"
                                th:each="i : ${#numbers.sequence(0, requestPage.totalPages - 1)}"
                                th:classappend="${i == requestPage.number} ? ' active'">
                                <a class="page-link"
                                   th:text="${i + 1}"
                                   th:href="@{/pt/trainer-requests(page=${i})}">
                                    1
                                </a>
                            </li>

                            <li class="page-item"
                                th:classappend="${requestPage.last} ? ' disabled'">
                                <a class="page-link"
                                   th:href="@{/pt/trainer-requests(page=${requestPage.number + 1})}">
                                    Sau
                                </a>
                            </li>

                        </ul>
                    </nav>

                </div>

            </div>
        </div>

    </div>

package com.utefit.cloudinary;

import com.utefit.dto.cloudinary.MediaUploadResult;
import com.utefit.service.CloudinaryService;
import com.utefit.validation.ImageValidator;

import com.utefit.config.CloudinaryConfig;
import com.utefit.service.impl.CloudinaryServiceImpl;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import org.springframework.mock.web.MockMultipartFile;

import org.springframework.test.context.junit.jupiter.SpringJUnitConfig;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.test.context.TestPropertySource;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assumptions;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

@SpringJUnitConfig(classes = {
        CloudinaryConfig.class,
        CloudinaryServiceImpl.class,
        ImageValidator.class
})
@TestPropertySource(properties = {
        "CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME=${CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME_ENV:}",
        "CLOUDINARY_API_KEY=${CLOUDINARY_API_KEY_ENV:}",
        "CLOUDINARY_API_SECRET=${CLOUDINARY_API_SECRET_ENV:}"
})
class CloudinaryIntegrationTest {

    @Autowired
    private CloudinaryService cloudinaryService;

    @Test
    void uploadImageSuccessfully() throws Exception {

        String imagePath =
                System.getenv("CLOUDINARY_TEST_IMAGE_PATH");

        Assumptions.assumeTrue(
                imagePath != null && !imagePath.isBlank(),
                "Chưa cấu hình CLOUDINARY_TEST_IMAGE_PATH"
        );

        Assumptions.assumeTrue(
                hasCloudinaryCredentials(),
                "Chưa cấu hình Cloudinary credentials"
        );

        Path path = Path.of(imagePath);

        Assumptions.assumeTrue(
                Files.isRegularFile(path),
                "Không tìm thấy ảnh kiểm thử"
        );

        String filename = path.getFileName().toString();

        String contentType =
                Files.probeContentType(path);

        if (contentType == null) {
            contentType = "image/jpeg";
        }

        MockMultipartFile file = new MockMultipartFile(
                "file",
                filename,
                contentType,
                Files.readAllBytes(path)
        );

        MediaUploadResult result =
                cloudinaryService.uploadImage(
                        file,
                        "utefit/trainers"
                );

        assertNotNull(result);
        assertNotNull(result.url());
        assertNotNull(result.publicId());

        assertTrue(
                result.url().startsWith("https://")
        );

        assertTrue(
                result.publicId().startsWith("utefit/trainers/")
        );

        System.out.println("UPLOAD SUCCESS");
        System.out.println("URL: " + result.url());
        System.out.println("PUBLIC ID: " + result.publicId());
    }

    private boolean hasCloudinaryCredentials() {

        return hasEnv("CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME_ENV")
                && hasEnv("CLOUDINARY_API_KEY_ENV")
                && hasEnv("CLOUDINARY_API_SECRET_ENV");
    }

    private boolean hasEnv(String name) {

        String value = System.getenv(name);

        return value != null && !value.isBlank();
    }
}
